📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

  • 本期基孔肯雅病毒研究集中呈现以下进展。
  • 衍生物6展现出最强的抗CHIKV活性,其有效浓度为低微摩尔级,选择指数适中,显著抑制病毒复制,保护细胞单层完整性,并降低病毒滴度,其病毒载量减少通过RT-qPCR得到确认。 [10.1007/s00284-026-05133-4]。
  • 患者出现严重呼吸系统症状,并确诊为基孔肯雅病毒感染,该感染与急性呼吸窘迫综合征的发生相关。 [10.7759/cureus.115063]。
  • 通过温度转换,悬浮适应性培养中的VLP产量提升了5倍,产量通过基于吸光度的方法进行定量。电子显微镜证实了VLPs的结构完整性。 [10.1038/s41540-026-00810-3]。
  • 结果显示,实验期间动物无死亡,感染动物出现显著病毒血症,并在腘淋巴结、股骨骨髓和肌肉中检测到可检测的病毒载量,证明CHIKV具有扩散并侵入多个靶组织的能力。此外,感染小鼠的总白细胞计数全面降低。
  • This reporting window highlights the following research developments.
  • Derivative 6 showed the strongest anti-CHIKV activity with low micromolar effective concentration and a moderate selectivity index, significantly reducing viral replication, preserving cell monolayer integrity, and decreasing viral titers as confirmed by RT-qPCR.
  • The patient exhibited severe respiratory symptoms and was confirmed to have chikungunya virus infection, which was linked to the development of acute respiratory distress syndrome. [10.7759/cureus.115063].
  • A 5-fold enhancement in VLP production was achieved in the suspension-adapted culture using the temperature shift, as quantified by an absorbance-based method.
  • Results revealed no deaths among the animals during the experimental period, and infected animals exhibited significant viremia as well as detectable viral loads in the popliteal lymph node, femoral bone marrow, and muscle, demonstrating CHIKV capacity to disseminate and reach multiple target tissues.

📰 本期新闻动态 / News Brief

  • 本期基孔肯雅病毒新闻集中呈现以下动态。
  • 一例罕见致死性基孔肯雅热相关急性呼吸窘迫综合征(ARDS)病例报告,患者为62岁男性,合并显著基础疾病,旅行后在印度勒克瑙确诊。该病例通过基孔肯雅病毒IgM抗体及RT-PCR检测确认。 [news-0b918990702cacbc]。
  • 西班牙基孔肯雅热病例激增,其中20例感染病例源自古巴。所有感染均为境外输入病例,西班牙境内未出现本地感染病例。病例数上升促使卫生当局加强对白纹伊蚊的监测,该蚊种为该疾病的主要传播媒介。 [news-f54cc4fe12149f3c]。
  • 巴伐利亚北欧公司已在巴西申请其基孔肯雅热疫苗疫苗的批准。该事件由ScanX新闻团队报道。 [news-670257f041f0ff0c]。
  • 西班牙已确认119例基孔肯雅热病例,其中20例与古巴相关。此次激增由境外输入病例引发,当局担心通过白纹伊蚊引发本地传播的风险。 [news-6a1bcb6f82836931]。
  • This reporting window highlights the following Chikungunya virus news developments.
  • A rare fatal case of chikungunya-associated acute respiratory distress syndrome (ARDS) was reported in a 62-year-old man with significant comorbidities, following travel to Lucknow, India.
  • Spain has experienced an increase in chikungunya cases, with 20 of the detected cases originating from Cuba. All infections are imported, and there are no locally originated cases within Spanish territory.
  • Bavarian Nordic has filed for approval of its chikungunya vaccine in Brazil. The event is reported by ScanX News Team. [news-670257f041f0ff0c].
  • Spain has identified 119 chikungunya cases, with 20 linked to Cuba. The outbreak is attributed to imported cases, and there is concern about the potential for local transmission through the tiger mosquito.

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: Bulletin of Mathematical Biology  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-08-19  |  DOI: 10.1007/s11538-026-01728-x  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 88, 9, 159

识别影响法属波利尼西亚与法属西印度群岛基孔肯雅热与寨卡病毒传播的关键因素:一种数据驱动的概率与数学建模方法

Identifying Key Factors in Predicting Chikungunya and Zika Transmission Across French Polynesia and the French West Indies: A Data-Driven Probabilistic and Mathematical Modeling Approach
作者： Zhiyuan Yu, Xi Huo, Peter J. Thomas, Qimin Huang
摘要中文翻译摘要 基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV)与寨卡病毒是由外温性埃及伊蚊传播的虫媒病毒,导致病毒感染呈现季节性传播模式。与监测数据和病例数据相结合的数学模型已被证明在理解虫媒病毒传播动态方面具有强大功效,但仍不清楚环境、媒介生态学与宿主生态学特征中哪些因素对准确捕捉传播动态与短期预测至关重要。我们以改进的SEIR分室模型为基线,将其扩展为与报告病例数据对齐的周度概率模型,并评估包含或排除蚊媒动态(M)、温度效应(T)与降水效应(P)的模型变体。我们将这些变体校准至法属波利尼西亚与法属西印度群岛九个地区的周度基孔肯雅热与寨卡病毒病例数据、温度与降水数据。我们采用基于AIC的模型比较评估拟合优度,采用滚动窗口预测比较预测性能与统计与空模型基线,并利用最佳拟合模型估计生物学意义明确的传播参数。纳入蚊媒动态、温度与降水可显著提升预测准确性,其中降水作为主要环境驱动因子,温度则作为调节因子。对应的SEI+M+T+P模型在预测准确性方面可与ARIMA媲美,同时提供机制可解释的预测。基孔肯雅病毒的传播性高于寨卡病毒,其区域异质性参数由不同的蚊媒-人传播率与蚊媒密度驱动,而寨卡病毒参数在各地区相对均一。研究结果表明,统一的媒介控制策略可能对寨卡病毒有效,而基孔肯雅热则需采用区域特异性策略。敏感性分析显示,快速检测与隔离症状个体是降低两种病毒传播的最有效措施之一。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在识别影响法属波利尼西亚与法属西印度群岛基孔肯雅热与寨卡病毒传播的关键因素。与监测数据和病例数据相结合的数学模型已被证明在理解虫媒病毒传播动态方面具有强大功效,但仍不清楚环境、媒介生态学与宿主生态学特征中哪些因素对准确捕捉传播动态与短期预测至关重要。
设计与对象
本研究采用数据驱动的概率与数学建模方法评估传播动态。研究纳入法属波利尼西亚与法属西印度群岛九个地区的周度基孔肯雅热与寨卡病毒病例数据、温度与降水数据。
核心方法
我们采用基于AIC的模型比较评估拟合优度,采用滚动窗口预测比较预测性能与统计与空模型基线,并利用最佳拟合模型估计生物学意义明确的传播参数。
主要结果
纳入蚊媒动态、温度与降水可显著提升预测准确性,其中降水作为主要环境驱动因子,温度则作为调节因子。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,统一的媒介控制策略可能对寨卡病毒有效,而基孔肯雅热则需采用区域特异性策略。敏感性分析显示,快速检测与隔离症状个体是降低两种病毒传播的最有效措施之一。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可为基孔肯雅热与寨卡病毒的监测与控制策略提供信息,强调环境因素与针对性干预在不同地区的重要性。
局限与证据强度
本研究受限于对报告病例数据的依赖,并假设基线模型的准确性。证据强度为中等,因结果基于模型校准与比较而非直接经验观察。

Identifying Key Factors in Predicting Chikungunya and Zika Transmission Across French Polynesia and the French West Indies: A Data-Driven Probabilistic and Mathematical Modeling Approach

Authors: Zhiyuan Yu, Xi Huo, Peter J. Thomas, Qimin Huang
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Chikungunya (CHIKV) and Zika are arboviruses transmitted by ectothermic Aedes mosquitoes, leading to seasonal outbreak patterns of viral infections. Mathematical models linked with vector and case data have proven powerful for understanding arboviral transmission dynamics, but it remains unclear if and which environmental, vector, and host features are essential for accurately capturing transmission dynamics and short-term forecasting. We began with a baseline modified SEIR compartmental model, extended it into a weekly probabilistic model aligned with reported surveillance data, and evaluated variants that include or exclude mosquito dynamics (M), temperature effects (T), and precipitation effects (P). We calibrated these variants to weekly CHIKV and Zika incidence, temperature, and precipitation data from nine regions in French Polynesia and the French West Indies. We used AIC-based model comparison to assess goodness-of-fit, rolling-window forecasting to compare predictive performance with statistical and null baselines, and the best-fitting model to estimate biologically meaningful transmission parameters. Incorporating mosquito dynamics, temperature, and precipitation substantially improves prediction accuracy, with precipitation acting as the primary environmental driver and temperature serving as a modulator. The corresponding SEI+M+T+P formulation achieves forecasting accuracy comparable to ARIMA while providing mechanistically interpretable predictions. CHIKV possesses higher transmissibility than Zika, with regionally heterogeneous parameters driven by varying mosquito-human transmission rates and mosquito densities, whereas Zika parameters are relatively homogeneous across regions. These findings suggest uniform vector control may be effective for Zika, whereas region-specific strategies are needed for CHIKV. Sensitivity analysis indicates that rapid diagnosis and isolation of symptomatic individuals are among the most effective measures for reducing transmission of both viruses.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to identify key factors in predicting Chikungunya and Zika transmission across French Polynesia and the French West Indies. Mathematical models linked with vector and case data have proven powerful for understanding arboviral transmission dynamics, but it remains unclear if and which environmental, vector, and host features are essential for accurately capturing transmission dynamics and short-term forecasting.
Design and population
A data-driven probabilistic and mathematical modeling approach was used to evaluate transmission dynamics. The study included weekly CHIKV and Zika incidence, temperature, and precipitation data from nine regions in French Polynesia and the French West Indies.
Core methods
We used AIC-based model comparison to assess goodness-of-fit, rolling-window forecasting to compare predictive performance with statistical and null baselines, and the best-fitting model to estimate biologically meaningful transmission parameters.
Main results
Incorporating mosquito dynamics, temperature, and precipitation substantially improves prediction accuracy, with precipitation acting as the primary environmental driver and temperature serving as a modulator.
Interpretation and novelty
These findings suggest uniform vector control may be effective for Zika, whereas region-specific strategies are needed for CHIKV. Sensitivity analysis indicates that rapid diagnosis and isolation of symptomatic individuals are among the most effective measures for reducing transmission of both viruses.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform surveillance and control strategies for Chikungunya and Zika, emphasizing the importance of environmental factors and targeted interventions in different regions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on reported surveillance data and assumes the accuracy of the baseline model. The evidence strength is moderate, as the results are based on model calibration and comparison rather than direct empirical observations.

4-氨基-7-氯喹啉衍生物体外抗基孔肯雅病毒活性的鉴定

Identification of 4-Amino-7-chloroquinoline Derivative with In Vitro Activity Against Chikungunya Virus
作者： Adriana Cotta Cardoso Reis, Camila Portruneli, Lívia da Cunha Agostini, Camila Rezende Minelli, Luana Neves Dias, Adalberto José de Lima, Breno de Mello Silva, Cíntia Lopes de Brito Magalhães, Glenda Nicioli da Silva, Guilherme Rocha Pereira
摘要中文翻译摘要 虫媒病毒如基孔肯雅病毒(基孔肯雅病毒,CHIKV)、寨卡病毒(ZIKV)和马亚罗病毒(MAYV)因缺乏有效的抗病毒药物疗法,已成为全球重大公共卫生挑战。喹啉类化合物已成为抗病毒药物开发的有前景骨架结构。本研究合成了五种4-氨基-7-氯喹啉衍生物,并通过体外实验在Vero细胞中评估其对CHIKV、ZIKV和MAYV的抗病毒活性。采用四唑盐法评估细胞毒性和抗病毒效果,并通过细胞病变抑制实验、病毒灭活实验及RT-qPCR法进行病毒载量定量分析。在测试的化合物中,衍生物6展现出最强的抗CHIKV活性,其有效浓度为低微摩尔级,选择指数适中。该化合物显著抑制病毒复制,保护细胞单层完整性,并降低病毒滴度,其病毒载量减少通过RT-qPCR得到确认。未观察到病毒灭活活性。相比之下,化合物6在病毒吸附后给药时抑制了病毒诱导的细胞病变效应,表明其抗病毒活性可能并非源于干扰病毒附着或吸附,而是源于抑制病毒复制周期的吸附后阶段。综上,研究结果凸显4-氨基-7-氯喹啉衍生物作为一类有前景的抗病毒药物,并将化合物6确定为CHIKV进一步开发的潜在先导化合物。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
虫媒病毒如基孔肯雅病毒(基孔肯雅病毒,CHIKV)因缺乏有效的抗病毒药物疗法,已成为全球公共卫生挑战,而喹啉类化合物被认为是具有前景的抗病毒药物骨架结构。
设计与对象
合成了五种4-氨基-7-氯喹啉衍生物,并通过体外实验在Vero细胞中评估其对CHIKV、寨卡病毒(ZIKV)和马亚罗病毒(MAYV)的抗病毒活性。
核心方法
采用四唑盐法评估细胞毒性和抗病毒效果,并通过细胞病变抑制实验、病毒灭活实验及RT-qPCR法进行病毒载量定量分析。
主要结果
衍生物6展现出最强的抗CHIKV活性,其有效浓度为低微摩尔级,选择指数适中,显著抑制病毒复制,保护细胞单层完整性,并降低病毒滴度,其病毒载量减少通过RT-qPCR得到确认。
解释与创新
化合物6在病毒吸附后给药时抑制了病毒诱导的细胞病变效应,表明其活性可能作用于复制周期的吸附后阶段,并被确定为CHIKV抗病毒药物开发的潜在先导化合物。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果支持4-氨基-7-氯喹啉衍生物(尤其是化合物6)作为CHIKV抗病毒药物的潜力,并建议进行进一步的临床前及临床评估。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于Vero细胞体外实验,未评估体内疗效或安全性,表明证据强度为中等,有待进一步验证。

Identification of 4-Amino-7-chloroquinoline Derivative with In Vitro Activity Against Chikungunya Virus

Authors: Adriana Cotta Cardoso Reis, Camila Portruneli, Lívia da Cunha Agostini, Camila Rezende Minelli, Luana Neves Dias, Adalberto José de Lima, Breno de Mello Silva, Cíntia Lopes de Brito Magalhães, Glenda Nicioli da Silva, Guilherme Rocha Pereira
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Arboviruses such as chikungunya virus (CHIKV), Zika virus (ZIKV), and Mayaro virus (MAYV) represent significant global health challenges due to the lack of effective antiviral therapies. Quinoline-based compounds have emerged as promising scaffolds for antiviral drug development. In this study, five 4-amino-7-chloroquinoline derivatives were synthesized and evaluated for their in vitro antiviral activity against CHIKV, ZIKV, and MAYV in Vero cells. Cytotoxicity and antiviral effects were assessed using a tetrazolium-based assay, followed by complementary analyses including cytopathic effect inhibition, virucidal assay, and viral load quantification by RT-qPCR. Among the tested compounds, derivative 6 demonstrated the most pronounced anti-CHIKV activity, with a low micromolar effective concentration and moderate selectivity index. This compound significantly reduced viral replication, preserved cell monolayer integrity, and decreased viral titers, achieving substantial viral load reduction confirmed by RT-qPCR. No virucidal activity was observed. In contrast, compound 6 inhibited the virus-induced cytopathic effect when administered after viral adsorption, suggesting that its antiviral activity is unlikely to result from interference with viral attachment or adsorption but rather from inhibition of a post-adsorption stage of the viral replication cycle. Overall, these findings highlight 4-amino-7-chloroquinoline derivatives as a promising class of antiviral agents and identify compound 6 as a potential lead candidate for further development against CHIKV.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Arboviruses such as chikungunya virus (CHIKV) pose global health challenges due to the absence of effective antiviral therapies, and quinoline-based compounds have been proposed as promising antiviral scaffolds.
Design and population
Five 4-amino-7-chloroquinoline derivatives were synthesized and evaluated for in vitro antiviral activity against CHIKV, Zika virus (ZIKV), and Mayaro virus (MAYV) using Vero cells.
Core methods
Cytotoxicity and antiviral effects were measured with a tetrazolium-based assay, cytopathic effect inhibition, a virucidal assay, and viral load quantification by RT-qPCR.
Main results
Derivative 6 showed the strongest anti-CHIKV activity with low micromolar effective concentration and a moderate selectivity index, significantly reducing viral replication, preserving cell monolayer integrity, and decreasing viral titers as confirmed by RT-qPCR.
Interpretation and novelty
Compound 6 inhibited virus-induced cytopathic effect post-adsorption, indicating activity at a post-attachment stage of the replication cycle, and was identified as a promising lead candidate for CHIKV antiviral development.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings support the potential of 4-amino-7-chloroquinoline derivatives, particularly compound 6, as antiviral agents for CHIKV, warranting further preclinical and clinical evaluation.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in vitro assays in Vero cells and does not assess in vivo efficacy or safety, indicating moderate evidence strength pending further validation.

Journal: Archives of Virology  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-08-20  |  DOI: 10.1007/s00705-026-06724-z  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 171, 9, 255
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

巴西基孔肯雅病毒分离株原发感染可保护免疫功能正常小鼠免于同源再次攻击后出现可检测的病毒血症

Primary infection with a Brazilian chikungunya virus isolate protects immunocompetent mice against detectable viremia following homologous rechallenge
作者： Naiara Soares da Silva, Mayanna Moreira Costa Fogaça, Elmo José dos Santos, Bruna Santos Souza da Silva, Ruth Dálety da Silva Brito, Rodrigo Lucas Ferreira do Carmo, Amanda da Rocha Santos, Milena Silva Souza, Tarcisio Macedo Silva, Eric Roberto Guimarães Rocha Aguiar
摘要中文翻译摘要 基孔肯雅病毒(基孔肯雅病毒,CHIKV)是一种节肢动物媒介病毒,可引起一种以严重多发性关节痛为特征的急性发热性疾病,主要累及外周关节。在大量患者中,这些症状可能进展为慢性表现,显著损害生活质量。因此,动物模型对于开发有效的治疗和预防策略(包括抗病毒药物和疫苗)至关重要。在此背景下,本研究旨在建立一种使用免疫功能正常C57BL/6小鼠的CHIKV感染实验模型。动物感染CHIKV后,随后监测其临床体征、病毒血症、病毒在组织中的扩散、总白细胞计数以及再感染易感性。结果显示,实验期间动物无死亡,感染动物出现显著病毒血症,并在腘淋巴结、股骨骨髓和肌肉中检测到可检测的病毒载量,证明CHIKV具有扩散并侵入多个靶组织的能力。此外,感染小鼠的总白细胞计数全面降低。值得注意的是,先前感染过CHIKV的动物对再感染具有抵抗力,表明建立了保护性免疫应答,并通过中和抗体的存在得到证实。综上所述,本研究建立的小鼠模型再现了CHIKV感染的关键特征,并为研究病毒发病机制以及评估新型抗病毒药物和疫苗候选物提供了可靠的平台。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV)是一种节肢动物媒介病毒,可引起一种以严重多发性关节痛为特征的急性发热性疾病,主要累及外周关节。在大量患者中,这些症状可能进展为慢性表现,显著损害生活质量。
设计与对象
本研究旨在建立一种使用免疫功能正常C57BL/6小鼠的CHIKV感染实验模型。动物感染CHIKV后,随后监测其临床体征、病毒血症、病毒在组织中的扩散、总白细胞计数以及再感染易感性。
核心方法
本研究通过感染免疫功能正常的C57BL/6小鼠并监测其临床体征、病毒血症、病毒在组织中的扩散、总白细胞计数以及再感染易感性来开展。
主要结果
结果显示,实验期间动物无死亡,感染动物出现显著病毒血症,并在腘淋巴结、股骨骨髓和肌肉中检测到可检测的病毒载量,证明CHIKV具有扩散并侵入多个靶组织的能力。此外,感染小鼠的总白细胞计数全面降低。
解释与创新
作者解读认为,本研究建立的小鼠模型再现了CHIKV感染的关键特征,并为研究病毒发病机制以及评估新型抗病毒药物和疫苗候选物提供了可靠的平台。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果为理解CHIKV感染并开发和测试新型抗病毒药物和疫苗候选物提供了宝贵的实验模型。
局限与证据强度
本研究未报告局限性,但证据基于一个控制严格且结果明确的实验模型,表明研究结果具有中等程度的可信度。

Primary infection with a Brazilian chikungunya virus isolate protects immunocompetent mice against detectable viremia following homologous rechallenge

Authors: Naiara Soares da Silva, Mayanna Moreira Costa Fogaça, Elmo José dos Santos, Bruna Santos Souza da Silva, Ruth Dálety da Silva Brito, Rodrigo Lucas Ferreira do Carmo, Amanda da Rocha Santos, Milena Silva Souza, Tarcisio Macedo Silva, Eric Roberto Guimarães Rocha Aguiar
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Chikungunya virus (CHIKV) is an arthropod-borne virus responsible for an acute febrile illness characterized by severe polyarthralgia, predominantly affecting peripheral joints. In a substantial proportion of patients, these symptoms may progress to chronic manifestations that significantly impair quality of life. Animal models are therefore critical for the development of effective therapeutic and preventive strategies, including antiviral agents and vaccines. In this context, the aim of this study was to establish an experimental model of CHIKV infection using immunocompetent C57BL/6 mice. Animals were infected with CHIKV and subsequently monitored for clinical signs, viremia, viral dissemination across tissues, total leukocyte counts, and susceptibility to reinfection. Results revealed no deaths among the animals during the experimental period, and infected animals exhibited significant viremia as well as detectable viral loads in the popliteal lymph node, femoral bone marrow, and muscle, demonstrating CHIKV capacity to disseminate and reach multiple target tissues. In addition, a global reduction in leukocyte counts was observed in infected mice. Notably, animals previously infected with CHIKV were resistant to reinfection, indicating the establishment of a protective immune response, as evidenced by the presence of neutralizing antibodies. In conclusion, the murine model established in this study recapitulates key features of CHIKV infection and provides a robust platform for investigating viral pathogenesis, as well as for evaluating novel antiviral strategies and vaccine candidates.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Chikungunya virus (CHIKV) is an arthropod-borne virus responsible for an acute febrile illness characterized by severe polyarthralgia, predominantly affecting peripheral joints. In a substantial proportion of patients, these symptoms may progress to chronic manifestations that significantly impair quality of life.
Design and population
The study aimed to establish an experimental model of CHIKV infection using immunocompetent C57BL/6 mice. Animals were infected with CHIKV and subsequently monitored for clinical signs, viremia, viral dissemination across tissues, total leukocyte counts, and susceptibility to reinfection.
Core methods
The study involved infecting immunocompetent C57BL/6 mice with CHIKV and monitoring them for clinical signs, viremia, viral dissemination across tissues, total leukocyte counts, and susceptibility to reinfection.
Main results
Results revealed no deaths among the animals during the experimental period, and infected animals exhibited significant viremia as well as detectable viral loads in the popliteal lymph node, femoral bone marrow, and muscle, demonstrating CHIKV capacity to disseminate and reach multiple target tissues. In addition, a global reduction in leukocyte counts was observed in infected mice.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that the murine model established in this study recapitulates key features of CHIKV infection and provides a robust platform for investigating viral pathogenesis, as well as for evaluating novel antiviral strategies and vaccine candidates.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide a valuable experimental model for understanding CHIKV infection and for developing and testing new antiviral strategies and vaccine candidates.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study does not report limitations, but the evidence is based on a well-controlled experimental model with clear outcomes, suggesting a moderate level of confidence in the findings.

慢性基孔肯雅热临床表型特征:里约热内卢一项观察性队列研究研究

Characterisation of the clinical forms of chronic chikungunya: an observational cohort study in Rio de Janeiro.
作者： Josephine Bourner, Alessandra Dantas do Nascimento, Wim Alexander Fleischmann, Jeni Stolow, Leonardo L S Bastos, Grazielle Viana Ramos, Patricia T Bozza, Thiago L Moreno Souza, Jan Felix Drexler, Jose Cerbino Neto
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在描述巴西急性和慢性基孔肯雅热感染的临床表现,并识别慢性基孔肯雅热的预测因子。科学与公共卫生问题在于需要了解基孔肯雅热感染的长期影响。
设计与对象
我们在里约热内卢Quinta D'Or医院急诊科开展了一项前瞜性观察性队列研究研究(2016年3月至2017年5月)。纳入确诊基孔肯雅热的患者。
核心方法
对疑似基孔肯雅热的成年患者进行基孔肯雅热、登革热和寨卡病毒的RT-PCR检测。基线时收集临床和人口统计学数据。在90天(D90)时,参与者通过电话随访评估持续症状、疼痛及生活质量。慢性疾病定义为D90时持续存在的关节痛或肌痛。
主要结果
Among 134 PCR-confirmed cases, 100 completed follow-up and 38% reported chronic manifestations. Female sex (adjusted OR 2.56, 95% CI 1.02-6.25) and increasing age (adjusted OR 1.04, 95% CI 1.01-1.07) were independently associated with chronic disease. Prostration and retro-orbital pain showed trends towards association.
解释与创新
作者解读认为,超过三分之一的实验室确诊基孔肯雅热成年患者在D90时出现持续症状,并对功能产生实质性影响。该发现强调需要针对性随访及将长期护理纳入虫媒病毒病管理。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可为慢性基孔肯雅热的监测策略制定提供参考,并指导将长期护理纳入虫媒病毒病管理。
局限与证据强度
本研究受限于依赖电话随访的自评数据,可能引入偏倚。由于观察性设计及潜在混杂因素,证据强度为中等。

Characterisation of the clinical forms of chronic chikungunya: an observational cohort study in Rio de Janeiro.

Authors: Josephine Bourner, Alessandra Dantas do Nascimento, Wim Alexander Fleischmann, Jeni Stolow, Leonardo L S Bastos, Grazielle Viana Ramos, Patricia T Bozza, Thiago L Moreno Souza, Jan Felix Drexler, Jose Cerbino Neto
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVES: This study aimed to characterise the clinical presentation of acute and chronic chikungunya virus infection in Brazil and identify predictors of chronic chikungunya. METHODS: We conducted a prospective observational cohort study in the emergency department of Hospital Quinta D'Or, Rio de Janeiro, between March 2016 and May 2017. Adults with suspected chikungunya were tested by RT-PCR for chikungunya, dengue, and Zika viruses. Patients with laboratory-confirmed chikungunya were enrolled. Clinical and demographic data were collected at baseline. At 90 days (D90), participants completed telephone follow-up assessing persistent symptoms, pain, and quality of life. Chronic disease was defined as ongoing arthralgia or myalgia at D90. RESULTS: Among 134 PCR-confirmed cases, 100 completed follow-up and 38% reported chronic manifestations. Female sex (adjusted OR 2.56, 95% CI 1.02-6.25) and increasing age (adjusted OR 1.04, 95% CI 1.01-1.07) were independently associated with chronic disease. Prostration and retro-orbital pain showed trends towards association. Chronic symptoms were associated with pain interference affecting mood, work, and sleep. CONCLUSIONS: Over one-third of adults with laboratory-confirmed chikungunya experienced persistent symptoms at D90, with substantial functional impact. Early identification of patients at risk may support targeted follow-up and integration of long-term care into arboviral disease management.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to characterise the clinical presentation of acute and chronic chikungunya virus infection in Brazil and identify predictors of chronic chikungunya. The scientific and public-health problem is the need to understand the long-term effects of chikungunya virus infection.
Design and population
We conducted a prospective observational cohort study in the emergency department of Hospital Quinta D'Or, Rio de Janeiro, between March 2016 and May 2017. Patients with laboratory-confirmed chikungunya were enrolled.
Core methods
Adults with suspected chikungunya were tested by RT-PCR for chikungunya, dengue, and Zika viruses. Clinical and demographic data were collected at baseline. At 90 days (D90), participants completed telephone follow-up assessing persistent symptoms, pain, and quality of life. Chronic disease was defined as ongoing arthralgia or myalgia at D90.
Main results
Among 134 PCR-confirmed cases, 100 completed follow-up and 38% reported chronic manifestations. Female sex (adjusted OR 2.56, 95% CI 1.02-6.25) and increasing age (adjusted OR 1.04, 95% CI 1.01-1.07) were independently associated with chronic disease. Prostration and retro-orbital pain showed trends towards association.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that over one-third of adults with laboratory-confirmed chikungunya experienced persistent symptoms at D90, with substantial functional impact. This finding highlights the need for targeted follow-up and integration of long-term care into arboviral disease management.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the development of surveillance strategies for chronic chikungunya and guide the integration of long-term care into the management of arboviral diseases.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on self-reported data from telephone follow-up, which may introduce bias. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the observational design and potential for confounding factors.

基于直接生物合成与筛选的基孔肯雅病毒 nsP2半胱氨酸蛋白酶共价抑制剂开发

Development of Covalent Inhibitors of Chikungunya Virus nsP2 Cysteine Protease Enabled by Direct-to-Biology Synthesis and Screening
作者： Zhengjun Cai, Kan Li, Sainetra Sridhar, Haozhou Tan, Hiwot A Demssie, Gaungjin Fan, Wenyi Zhang, Bobby Brooke Herrera, Jun Wang
摘要中文翻译摘要 基孔肯雅病毒(基孔肯雅病毒,CHIKV)是一种由节肢动物传播的甲病毒,因其快速扩散和缺乏有效的抗病毒药物疗法而成为全球公共卫生威胁。病毒编码的非结构蛋白2(nsP2)的半胱氨酸蛋白酶活性对CHIKV复制至关重要,因其可加工病毒多聚蛋白并对抗宿主抗病毒免疫应答,使其成为极具吸引力的治疗靶点。本研究提出一种快速药物开发平台,该平台整合了共价对接与直接生物(D2B)合成和筛选,用于识别nsP2抑制剂。通过计算机辅助对接优选的候选化合物无需纯化即直接合成并采用FRET酶学检测进行验证。该方法识别出多个具有多样化化学骨架、强效酶抑制活性和体外抗病毒活性的nsP2抑制剂。这些发现共同建立了一种简化的共价抑制剂开发策略,并为CHIKV抗病毒药物研发提供了有前景的先导化合物。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV)因其快速扩散和缺乏有效的抗病毒药物疗法而构成全球公共卫生威胁,亟需治疗靶点和干预措施。本研究聚焦于病毒编码的非结构蛋白2(nsP2)的半胱氨酸蛋白酶活性,该活性对病毒复制和对抗宿主免疫应答至关重要,从而弥补了抗病毒药物选择的不足。
设计与对象
本研究采用了一种快速药物开发平台,该平台整合了共价对接与直接生物(D2B)合成和筛选,用于识别nsP2抑制剂。通过计算机辅助对接优选的候选化合物无需纯化即直接合成并采用FRET酶学检测进行验证。
核心方法
本研究利用共价对接优选候选抑制剂,随后进行直接生物合成与筛选。这些候选化合物随后通过FRET酶学检测评估其对CHIKV nsP2半胱氨酸蛋白酶的抑制活性。
主要结果
该方法识别出多个具有多样化化学骨架的nsP2抑制剂,展现出强效的酶抑制活性和体外抗病毒活性。
解释与创新
研究结果建立了一种针对CHIKV nsP2的共价抑制剂开发的简化策略,为抗病毒药物疗法提供了有前景的先导化合物。该方法绕过了传统纯化步骤,加速了活性化合物的识别过程。
科研与公卫意义
本研究提供了一种快速平台用于抗病毒药物发现,有望缩短CHIKV治疗药物开发的时间线。所识别的抑制剂代表了进一步进行临床前和临床评估的潜在候选药物。
局限与证据强度
现有证据仅限于体外酶学和体外抗病毒活性检测,缺乏体内验证,限制了研究结论的强度。需要进一步研究以评估药代动力学、毒性和动物模型中的疗效。

Development of Covalent Inhibitors of Chikungunya Virus nsP2 Cysteine Protease Enabled by Direct-to-Biology Synthesis and Screening

Authors: Zhengjun Cai, Kan Li, Sainetra Sridhar, Haozhou Tan, Hiwot A Demssie, Gaungjin Fan, Wenyi Zhang, Bobby Brooke Herrera, Jun Wang
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Chikungunya virus (CHIKV), an arthropod-borne alphavirus, has emerged as a global health threat due to its rapid transmission and the lack of effective antiviral therapies. The cysteine protease activity of the virus-encoded nonstructural protein 2 (nsP2) is critical for CHIKV replication, as it processes viral polyproteins and counteracts host antiviral defenses, establishing it as a highly attractive target for therapeutic intervention. In this study, we present a rapid drug development platform that integrates covalent docking with direct-to-biology (D2B) synthesis and screening to identify nsP2 inhibitors. Candidates prioritized by in silico docking were synthesized and directly tested in FRET enzymatic assays without purification. This approach led to the identification of several nsP2 inhibitors with diverse chemical scaffolds, potent enzymatic inhibition, and antiviral activity. Together, these findings establish a streamlined strategy for covalent inhibitor development and provide promising leads for CHIKV antiviral development.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Chikungunya virus (CHIKV) poses a global health threat due to rapid transmission and the absence of effective antiviral therapies, creating an urgent need for therapeutic targets and interventions. The study addresses the lack of antiviral options by focusing on the cysteine protease activity of the virus-encoded nonstructural protein 2 (nsP2), which is essential for viral replication and host immune evasion.
Design and population
The study employed a rapid drug development platform integrating covalent docking with direct-to-biology (D2B) synthesis and screening to identify nsP2 inhibitors. Candidates prioritized by in silico docking were synthesized and directly tested in FRET enzymatic assays without purification.
Core methods
The research utilized covalent docking to prioritize candidate inhibitors, followed by direct-to-biology synthesis and screening. These candidates were then evaluated using FRET enzymatic assays to assess their inhibitory activity against the CHIKV nsP2 cysteine protease.
Main results
The approach identified several nsP2 inhibitors with diverse chemical scaffolds, demonstrating potent enzymatic inhibition and antiviral activity.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings establish a streamlined strategy for covalent inhibitor development against CHIKV nsP2, providing promising leads for antiviral therapy. This method bypasses traditional purification steps, accelerating the identification of active compounds.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study offers a rapid platform for antiviral drug discovery that could shorten timelines for CHIKV therapeutic development. The identified inhibitors represent potential candidates for further preclinical and clinical evaluation.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited to in vitro enzymatic and antiviral assays without in vivo validation, which constrains the strength of the findings. Additional studies are needed to assess pharmacokinetics, toxicity, and efficacy in animal models.

蚊子唾液亚单位 疫苗 增强 VLP 疫苗 对寨卡病毒和 基孔肯雅病毒 的保护效果

Mosquito Saliva Subunit Vaccine Enhances Efficacy of VLP Vaccines Against Zika and Chikungunya Viruses
作者： Siân Jossi, Megan E. Cole, Yonca Keskek Turk, Sam Verwimp, Katrien Trappeniers, James Luk, Joshua X. D. Ang, Luke Alphey, Leen Delang, Clive S. McKimmie
摘要中文翻译背景/目的:蚊媒病毒可导致全球显著发病率和死亡率。蚊虫叮咬时分泌的唾液可促进吸血并增强病毒感染。本研究评估了将病毒特异性抗原与蚊虫唾液蛋白衍生肽段联合使用是否可提高对Zika病毒(ZIKV)和基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV)小鼠模型的保护效果。方法:基于保守性、预测的T细胞反应性以及小鼠细胞和体液免疫原性,从埃及伊蚊和库蚊唾液蛋白中筛选肽段。小鼠以含佐剂的唾液肽段(SAL)、商业化ZIKV或CHIKV样颗粒(VLP)或唾液肽段与相应VLP联合进行皮下免疫,初免后间隔两周加强免疫。每剂含0.5 μg VLP和/或10 nmol各唾液肽段,以Montanide ISA-51佐剂制备;联合组中VLP与SAL分别注射于小鼠两侧。加强免疫后两周,小鼠以ZIKV或CHIKV加埃及伊蚊唾液进行攻击。攻击实验组在数据分析完成前对分组信息实施盲法。结果:SAL可增强VLP对疫苗接种的保护效果。在CHIKV模型中,CHIKV VLP + SAL可预防关节肿胀,并降低接种踝部的感染性病毒量及对侧踝部的病毒RNA水平。在ZIKV模型中,ZIKV VLP + SAL可降低皮肤和脑组织中的病毒RNA水平并提高存活率。结论:蚊虫唾液肽段抗原可提高VLP对ZIKV和CHIKV小鼠模型的保护效果。唾液抗原或可为多种由埃及伊蚊和库蚊传播的虫媒病毒提供补充性疫苗成分。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
背景/目标:蚊媒病毒在全球范围内造成显著的发病率和 死亡率。本研究旨在解决针对这些病毒改进 疫苗 策略的需求。
设计与对象
本研究评估了将病毒特异性抗原与蚊子唾液蛋白衍生肽结合在寨卡病毒(ZIKV)和 基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV)小鼠模型中的效果。小鼠通过皮下免疫接种,使用佐剂唾液肽(SAL)、商业的 ZIKV 或 CHIKV 类颗粒(VLPs)或与相关 VLPs 结合的唾液肽进行初次和加强免疫。
核心方法
根据保守性、预测的T细胞反应性和小鼠的细胞和体液免疫原性,选择了来自 Aedes 和 Culex 唾液蛋白的肽。疫苗 剂量包含 0.5 μg VLPs 和/或每种唾液肽 10 nmol,使用 Montanide ISA-51 配制;当结合使用时,VLPs 和 SAL 分别在相反的侧进行接种。挑战团队在数据分析完成前不知道分组分配。
主要结果
SAL 增强了 VLP 疫苗接种 的保护效果,与单独使用 VLPs 相比。在 CHIKV 模型中,CHIKV VLP + SAL 防止了关节肿胀,并减少了接种踝部的感染病毒量和对侧踝部的病毒RNA。在 ZIKV 模型中,ZIKV VLP + SAL 减少了皮肤和脑组织中的病毒RNA并提高了存活率。
解释与创新
作者认为,蚊子唾液肽抗原提高了 VLP 疫苗 对 ZIKV 和 CHIKV 在小鼠模型中的保护效果。这一发现代表了一种增强 疫苗 对虫媒病毒效果的新方法。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,唾液抗原可能为由 Aedes 和 Culex 蚊子传播的多种虫媒病毒提供互补的 疫苗 组件,从而改善 监测 和预防策略。
局限与证据强度
本研究是在小鼠模型中进行的,这可能限制其在人类中的普遍适用性。证据强度中等,因为结果基于实验模型,而非临床试验。

Mosquito Saliva Subunit Vaccine Enhances Efficacy of VLP Vaccines Against Zika and Chikungunya Viruses

Authors: Siân Jossi, Megan E. Cole, Yonca Keskek Turk, Sam Verwimp, Katrien Trappeniers, James Luk, Joshua X. D. Ang, Luke Alphey, Leen Delang, Clive S. McKimmie
Original abstract (English)
Background/Objectives: Mosquito-borne viruses cause substantial global morbidity and mortality. Mosquito saliva, secreted during biting, facilitates blood feeding and can enhance virus infection. Here, we evaluated whether combining virus-specific antigens with mosquito salivary protein-derived peptides improves protection in Zika virus (ZIKV) and chikungunya virus (CHIKV) mouse models. Methods: Peptides from Aedes and Culex salivary proteins were selected based on conservation, predicted T-cell reactivity, and cellular and humoral immunogenicity in mice. Mice were immunised subcutaneously with a prime and boost two weeks apart using adjuvanted salivary peptides (SAL), commercial ZIKV- or CHIKV-like particles (VLPs), or salivary peptides combined with the relevant VLPs. Vaccine doses contained 0.5 μg VLPs and/or 10 nmol of each salivary peptide, formulated with Montanide ISA-51; when combined, VLPs and SAL were administered on opposite flanks. Two weeks after boosting, mice were challenged with ZIKV or CHIKV plus Aedes aegypti saliva. Challenge teams were blinded to group allocation until data analyses were complete. Results: SAL enhanced the protective effect of VLP vaccination compared with VLPs alone. In the CHIKV model, CHIKV VLP + SAL prevented joint swelling and reduced infectious virus in the inoculated ankle and viral RNA in the contralateral ankle. In the ZIKV model, ZIKV VLP + SAL reduced viral RNA in skin and brain tissue and improved survival. Conclusions: Mosquito salivary peptide antigens improved the protective efficacy of VLP vaccines against ZIKV and CHIKV in mouse models. Salivary antigens may provide a complementary vaccine component for multiple arboviruses transmitted by Aedes and Culex mosquitoes.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Background/Objectives: Mosquito-borne viruses cause substantial global morbidity and mortality. The study aimed to address the need for improved vaccine strategies against these viruses.
Design and population
The study evaluated the efficacy of combining virus-specific antigens with mosquito salivary protein-derived peptides in Zika virus (ZIKV) and chikungunya virus (CHIKV) mouse models. Mice were immunised with adjuvanted salivary peptides (SAL), commercial ZIKV- or CHIKV-like particles (VLPs), or salivary peptides combined with the relevant VLPs.
Core methods
Peptides from Aedes and Culex salivary proteins were selected based on conservation, predicted T-cell reactivity, and cellular and humoral immunogenicity in mice. Vaccine doses contained 0.5 μg VLPs and/or 10 nmol of each salivary peptide, formulated with Montanide ISA-51. When combined, VLPs and SAL were administered on opposite flanks. Challenge teams were blinded to group allocation until data analyses were complete.
Main results
SAL enhanced the protective effect of VLP vaccination compared with VLPs alone. In the CHIKV model, CHIKV VLP + SAL prevented joint swelling and reduced infectious virus in the inoculated ankle and viral RNA in the contralateral ankle. In the ZIKV model, ZIKV VLP + SAL reduced viral RNA in skin and brain tissue and improved survival.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that mosquito salivary peptide antigens improved the protective efficacy of VLP vaccines against ZIKV and CHIKV in mouse models. This finding represents a novel approach to enhancing vaccine effectiveness against arboviruses.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that salivary antigens may provide a complementary vaccine component for multiple arboviruses transmitted by Aedes and Culex mosquitoes, offering potential improvements in surveillance and prevention strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was conducted in mouse models, which may limit generalisability to humans. The evidence is moderate, as the results are based on experimental models and not clinical trials.

热浪与降水对基孔肯雅热传播动态的影响

Influence of Heat Wave and Precipitation on Chikungunya Transmission
作者： Wei Yan, Jianping Huang, Shujuan Hu, Xiaolu Fan, Xinbo Lian
摘要中文翻译摘要 日益频发的极端天气事件正在加剧蚊媒疾病的发生风险,对人类健康构成显著威胁。然而,极端天气如何影响蚊媒疾病的传播动态仍有待深入理解。本研究将热浪与有效降水作为参数化方案,纳入SEI-SEIR动态模型,以量化其对基孔肯雅热传播动态的驱动作用。基于2025年佛山某次基孔肯雅热暴发数据,该模型成功复现了观测到的传播动态。归因分析显示,热浪对传播动态产生双重效应:蚊媒适宜性下降9.7%,但病毒复制加速与人类行为改变产生正向驱动力,最终使感染病例净增加76%。有效降水对传播规模的调控呈非线性特征:分散性降水使蚊媒数量增长205%,并将传播强度放大10.5%。本研究为极端天气影响蚊媒传染病的理解提供了新视角。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在探明极端天气事件如何影响蚊媒疾病(重点为基孔肯雅热)的传播动态,因极端天气事件频率上升正在加剧蚊媒疾病的发生风险,并对人类健康构成显著威胁。
设计与对象
研究将热浪与有效降水作为参数化方案,纳入SEI-SEIR动态模型,以量化其对基孔肯雅热传播动态的驱动作用,并基于2025年佛山某次基孔肯雅热暴发数据进行模拟。
核心方法
研究团队采用SEI-SEIR动态模型,将热浪与有效降水作为参数化方案,用于模拟并量化极端天气对基孔肯雅热传播动态的影响。
主要结果
归因分析显示,热浪对传播动态产生双重效应:蚊媒适宜性下降9.7%,但病毒复制加速与人类行为改变产生正向驱动力,最终使感染病例净增加76%。有效降水对传播规模的调控呈非线性特征:分散性降水使蚊媒数量增长205%,并将传播强度放大10.5%。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,极端天气事件可显著影响基孔肯雅热传播动态,其中热浪具有复杂的双重效应,而有效降水在传播动态中发挥非线性调控作用。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果揭示了气候因素如何影响基孔肯雅热传播动态,可为公共卫生策略制定、风险评估及极端天气事件期间的干预措施提供科学依据。
局限与证据强度
本研究基于2025年佛山某次基孔肯雅热暴发数据构建模型,可能限制结果的普适性;且证据基于观测到的传播动态,可能无法涵盖所有现实复杂性。

Influence of Heat Wave and Precipitation on Chikungunya Transmission

Authors: Wei Yan, Jianping Huang, Shujuan Hu, Xiaolu Fan, Xinbo Lian
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Increasingly frequent extreme weather events are heightening the chances of mosquito‐borne diseases outbreaks, posing a notable threat to human health. However, how extreme weather influences the transmission dynamics of mosquito‐borne diseases remains poorly understood. Here, we integrate heat waves and effective precipitation as parameterization schemes into an SEI‐SEIR dynamic model to quantify their driving effects on chikungunya transmission. Based on the 2025 chikungunya fever outbreak in Foshan, the model successfully reproduced the observed transmission dynamics. Attribution analysis revealed that heat waves exerted a dual effect on transmission. Mosquito suitability declined by 9.7%, yet accelerated viral replication and altered human behavior generated a positive forcing that drove a net 76% increase in infected cases. Effective precipitation regulated outbreak scale nonlinearly, dispersed rainfall increased mosquito abundance by 205% and amplified transmission by 10.5%. Our research offered new insights into the impact of extreme weather on mosquito‐borne infectious diseases.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand how extreme weather events influence the transmission dynamics of mosquito-borne diseases, particularly chikungunya, as increasing frequency of such events is heightening the chances of outbreaks, posing a notable threat to human health.
Design and population
The study integrated heat waves and effective precipitation into an SEI-SEIR dynamic model to quantify their driving effects on chikungunya transmission, using data from the 2025 chikungunya fever outbreak in Foshan.
Core methods
The researchers used an SEI-SEIR dynamic model with heat waves and effective precipitation as parameterization schemes to simulate and quantify the impact of extreme weather on chikungunya transmission dynamics.
Main results
Attribution analysis revealed that heat waves exerted a dual effect on transmission, with mosquito suitability declining by 9.7%, yet accelerated viral replication and altered human behavior generated a positive forcing that drove a net 76% increase in infected cases. Effective precipitation regulated outbreak scale nonlinearly, with dispersed rainfall increasing mosquito abundance by 205% and amplifying transmission by 10.5%.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrated that extreme weather events can significantly influence chikungunya transmission, with heat waves having a complex dual effect and effective precipitation playing a nonlinear role in outbreak dynamics.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results provide insights into how climate factors affect chikungunya transmission, which can inform public health strategies for surveillance, risk assessment, and intervention during extreme weather events.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study relied on a model based on the 2025 chikungunya fever outbreak in Foshan, which may limit generalizability, and the evidence is based on observed transmission dynamics, which may not capture all real-world complexities.

刚果民主共和国寨卡病毒和基孔肯雅病毒的流行病学考虑

Dengue and chikungunya virus epidemiology and vaccine considerations in the Democratic Republic of the Congo
作者： Rachel Rose Jablonski Sendor
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
寨卡病毒(DENV)和基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV)在非洲正日益被认识到是重要的公共卫生威胁,但其流行病学特征仍不明确,部分原因是诊断手段有限,以及与高度流行的疾病如疟疾的临床症状重叠,使得疾病鉴别变得复杂。
设计与对象
在刚果民主共和国(DRC),已有基孔肯雅病毒的流行证据,但其传播动态和流行特征仍不确定。此外,非洲国家在寨卡病毒疫苗试验中的缺席限制了对这些地区疫苗效果的理解,因为这些地区的病毒传播动态、基线风险、人口特征、流行血清型以及共流行的黄病毒可能与试验人群不同。
核心方法
在目标1中,我们分析了刚果民主共和国金沙萨1250名儿童和成人样本,使用抗原捕获ELISA方法检测到38.1%(95% CI: 34.5%-41.8%)的寨卡病毒IgG血清流行率,以及24.2%(95% CI: 21.1%-27.6%)的基孔肯雅病毒IgG血清流行率。催化感染力模型(FOI)显示寨卡病毒的持续传播(FOI: 0.022 [95% CrI: 0.020–0.025]),以及基孔肯雅病毒的偶发传播,且两种病毒之间存在显著的空间异质性。
主要结果
血清流行率在年龄和地理位置上存在差异。
解释与创新
这些发现表明,基孔肯雅病毒和寨卡病毒在金沙萨普遍存在,具有空间异质性和持续传播,同时展示了可迁移性方法如何在研究较少、低流行水平的环境中实际生成疫苗影响的证据。
科研与公卫意义
结果强调了加强刚果民主共和国的虫媒病毒监测的必要性,并代表了在非洲低流行人群中填补寨卡病毒疫苗证据空白的第一步。
局限与证据强度
提供的证据未报告与基孔肯雅病毒研究设计或数据收集相关的具体局限性。

Dengue and chikungunya virus epidemiology and vaccine considerations in the Democratic Republic of the Congo

Authors: Rachel Rose Jablonski Sendor
Original abstract (English)
Dengue virus (DENV) and chikungunya virus (CHIKV) are increasingly recognized as important public health threats across Africa, yet their epidemiology is poorly understood, partly due to limited surveillance and diagnostics, and overlapping clinical symptoms with highly prevalent illnesses such as malaria, complicating disease differentiation. In the Democratic Republic of the Congo (DRC), evidence of transmission exists, but prevalence and transmission dynamics remain uncertain. Further, absence of African countries in dengue vaccine trials limits understanding of expected vaccine performance in these settings, where transmission dynamics, baseline risks, demographics, circulating serotypes, and co-circulating flaviviruses likely differ from trial populations. In the DRC, documented outbreaks and past infections, alongside limited epidemiological data highlight the need to quantify disease burden and identify at-risk populations to inform intervention strategies. In Aim 1, we analyzed samples from a cross-sectional study of 1,250 children and adults in Kinshasa, DRC using antigen-capture ELISA, and detected a 38.1% (95% CI: 34.5%-41.8%) seroprevalence of DENV IgG, and 24.2% (95% CI: 21.1%-27.6%) seroprevalence of CHIKV IgG. Seroprevalences varied by age and location. Catalytic force of infection (FOI) modeling indicated sustained DENV transmission (FOI: 0.022 [95% CrI: 0.020–0.025]), and sporadic CHIKV transmission, with distinct spatial heterogeneity between viruses. In Aim 2, we applied FOI estimates to model DENV vaccine impact in Kinshasa, representing a lower-endemicity setting, using epidemiological transportability methods within a Monte Carlo simulation calibrated to TAK-003 vaccine Phase 3 trial data and Kinshasa population inputs. Trial-derived hazard ratios were transported across effect modifiers under varying uncertainty scenarios. Overall vaccine effectiveness was similar between simulated trial and target populations in primary analyses; results among children aged 4-5 years suggested uncertainty in vaccine impact, and the number needed to vaccinate was higher in the target population, reflecting lower baseline DENV risks. These findings demonstrate DENV and CHIKV are prevalent in Kinshasa, with spatial heterogeneity and ongoing transmission, and demonstrate how transportability methods could pragmatically generate evidence of vaccine impact in understudied, lower-endemicity settings. Results underscore a need to strengthen arboviral surveillance in the DRC and represent first steps towards addressing gaps in dengue vaccine evidence within lower-endemicity African populations.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue virus (DENV) and chikungunya virus (CHIKV) are increasingly recognized as important public health threats across Africa, yet their epidemiology is poorly understood, partly due to limited surveillance and diagnostics, and overlapping clinical symptoms with highly prevalent illnesses such as malaria, complicating disease differentiation.
Design and population
In the Democratic Republic of the Congo (DRC), evidence of transmission exists, but prevalence and transmission dynamics remain uncertain. Catalytic force of infection (FOI) modeling indicated sustained DENV transmission (FOI: 0.022 [95% CrI: 0.020–0.025]), and sporadic CHIKV transmission, with distinct spatial heterogeneity between viruses.

Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations related to the study design or data collection for CHIKV.

基孔肯雅热与登革热的代谢特征:一项比较研究

Metabolic Signatures of Chikungunya Versus Dengue: A Comparative Study
作者： Yuqiu Liu, Meiyi Li, Huili Chen, Xi Liu
摘要中文翻译背景:基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV)与登革病毒是全球最具重要临床意义的蚊媒病原体之一,在流行地区造成了巨大的疾病负担。尽管两者症状重叠,但其预后却明显不同。这种差异凸显了区分这两种疾病并阐明其不同发病机制的关键需求。方法:本研究采用液相色谱-串联质谱法开展比较代谢组学分析,系统比较感染者血浆代谢谱。结果:我们收集了26例基孔肯雅热(CHIKF)患者、49例登革热(DF)患者及28名健康个体的血浆样本。临床上,CHIKF患者出现皮疹的比例(96.3% vs 36.7%)及小关节受累更高,而DF患者则表现为发热时间更长且血液学异常更严重。代谢组学分析显示,与健康对照组相比,两组感染者在特定代谢物(如丁香酚硫酸盐、溶血磷脂酰胆碱(14:0/0:0)和胡椒碱)方面存在显著差异。京都基因与基因组百科全书(KEGG)通路分析突出了CHIKF独特的代谢扰动,如过氧化物酶体增殖物激活受体(PPAR)信号通路和机械靶向雷帕霉素复合体(mTOR)通路,提示潜在的CHIKV诱导性关节炎发病机制。结论:本研究识别出与CHIKF和DF相关的不同代谢特征及功能通路,为差异化诊断与治疗靶点提供潜在机制。丁香酚硫酸盐在调节CHIKV诱导性关节痛中的作用尤为值得关注,其已知的抗炎特性为此提供了支持。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV)与登革病毒在临床表现重叠但预后迥异,给流行地区造成了巨大疾病负担,亟需工具区分这两种感染并理解其不同发病机制。
设计与对象
本比较代谢组学研究收集了26例基孔肯雅热(CHIKF)患者、49例登革热(DF)患者及28名健康个体的血浆样本,以比较其代谢谱。
核心方法
采用液相色谱-串联质谱法开展比较代谢组学分析,系统比较三组血浆代谢物谱。
主要结果
CHIKF患者出现皮疹的比例(96.3% vs 36.7%)及小关节受累更高,而DF患者则表现为发热时间更长且血液学异常更严重。与健康对照组相比,两组感染者在特定代谢物(如丁香酚硫酸盐、溶血磷脂酰胆碱(14:0/0:0)和胡椒碱)方面存在显著差异。
解释与创新
本研究识别出与CHIKF相关的不同代谢特征及功能通路,包括PPAR信号通路和mTOR通路的扰动,提示潜在的CHIKV诱导性关节炎发病机制,并为差异化诊断与治疗靶点提供新思路。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果突出了区分基孔肯雅热与登革热的潜在生物标志物,并提示可能的代谢通路,为针对CHIKV诱导性关节痛的治疗策略提供参考。
局限与证据强度
本研究受限于样本量较小且缺乏纵向随访,限制了结果的普遍性与因果推断;由于这些限制,证据强度被评估为中等。

Metabolic Signatures of Chikungunya Versus Dengue: A Comparative Study

Authors: Yuqiu Liu, Meiyi Li, Huili Chen, Xi Liu
Original abstract (English)
Background: Chikungunya virus and dengue virus rank among the most clinically significant mosquito-borne pathogens worldwide, imposing substantial disease burdens in endemic regions. Despite their overlapping symptomatology, the diseases diverge markedly in prognosis. This disparity underscores the critical need for tools to differentiate these conditions and elucidate their distinct pathogenic mechanisms. Method: We conducted liquid chromatography–tandem mass spectrometry-based comparative metabolomic analyses to systematically compare plasma profiles from infected individuals. Results: We collected plasma samples from 26 chikungunya fever (CHIKF) patients, 49 dengue fever (DF) patients and 28 healthy individuals. Clinically, CHIKF patients exhibited a higher prevalence of rash (96.3% vs. 36.7%) and involvement of small joints, while DF patients showed prolonged fever and more severe hematological abnormalities. Metabolically, significant differences were observed in specific metabolites, such as eugenol sulfate, lysophosphatidylcholine (14:0/0:0), and piperine, which were dysregulated in both groups compared to healthy controls. A Kyoto Encyclopedia of Genes and Genomes (KEGG) pathway analysis highlighted unique metabolic perturbations in CHIKF, such as peroxisome proliferator-activated receptor (PPAR) signaling and mechanistic target of rapamycin complex (mTOR) pathways, suggesting potential mechanisms underlying CHIKV-induced arthritis. Conclusions: This study identifies distinct metabolic signatures and functional pathways associated with CHIKF and DF, offering a potential mechanism for differential diagnosis and therapeutic targets. The role of eugenol sulfate in modulating CHIKV-induced arthralgia is particularly promising, supported by its known anti-inflammatory properties.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Chikungunya virus (CHIKV) and dengue virus impose substantial disease burdens with overlapping clinical presentations but divergent prognoses, creating a need for tools to differentiate these infections and understand their distinct pathogenic mechanisms.
Design and population
This comparative metabolomic study collected plasma samples from 26 chikungunya fever (CHIKF) patients, 49 dengue fever (DF) patients, and 28 healthy individuals to compare metabolic profiles.
Core methods
Liquid chromatography–tandem mass spectrometry-based comparative metabolomic analyses were performed to systematically compare plasma metabolite profiles across the three groups.
Main results
CHIKF patients exhibited a higher prevalence of rash (96.3% vs. 36.7%) and involvement of small joints, while DF patients showed prolonged fever and more severe hematological abnormalities. Significant differences were observed in specific metabolites such as eugenol sulfate, lysophosphatidylcholine (14:0/0:0), and piperine, which were dysregulated in both infected groups compared to healthy controls.
Interpretation and novelty
The study identifies distinct metabolic signatures and functional pathways associated with CHIKF, including perturbations in PPAR signaling and mTOR pathways, suggesting potential mechanisms underlying CHIKV-induced arthritis and offering avenues for differential diagnosis and therapeutic targets.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight potential biomarkers for differentiating chikungunya from dengue and suggest metabolic pathways that may inform therapeutic strategies targeting CHIKV-induced arthralgia.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its small sample size and lack of longitudinal follow-up, which constrains generalizability and causal inference; the evidence strength is assessed as moderate due to these constraints.

2008–2024年ENSO对全球基孔肯雅热传播的影响:一项多国分布式滞后时间序列分析

ENSO Impact on Global Chikungunya Virus Transmission, 2008–2024: A Multi-Country Distributed-Lag Time-Series Analysis
作者： Shi-Hui Shan, Long-Tao Chen, Wen-Qi Xie, Chen-Long Lv, Dong Jiang, Fang-Yu Ding, Gang Dong, Li-Qun Fang, Ding Fang-yu
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
基孔肯雅热正在全球扩散,但厄尔尼诺-南方涛动(ENSO)如何影响其传播仍不清楚。
设计与对象
本研究旨在通过温度和降水遥相关评估ENSO不同相位对基孔肯雅热传播的影响,并基于气候变化预测未来风险负担。
核心方法
我们汇编了2008–2024年各国和次国家级基孔肯雅热病例数据,采用E指数(东太平洋厄尔尼诺)和C指数(中太平洋拉尼娜)量化ENSO-气候遥相关,并应用分布式滞后时间序列模型评估ENSO通过遥相关对基孔肯雅热传播的影响。
主要结果
Modelling shows that El Niño increased chikungunya risk after a 2-year lag (cumulative relative risk [CRR] = 1.40, 95% confidence interval [CI]: 1.02–1.85), while La Niña suppressed it (CRR = 0.09, 95% CI: 0.07–0.13).
解释与创新
ENSO通过不对称且空间异质的遥相关途径影响基孔肯雅热传播。
科研与公卫意义
将ENSO预测纳入监测工作有望提升气候敏感地区的疾病准备能力。
局限与证据强度
温度遥相关是影响效应空间异质性的主要调节因素。

ENSO Impact on Global Chikungunya Virus Transmission, 2008–2024: A Multi-Country Distributed-Lag Time-Series Analysis

Authors: Shi-Hui Shan, Long-Tao Chen, Wen-Qi Xie, Chen-Long Lv, Dong Jiang, Fang-Yu Ding, Gang Dong, Li-Qun Fang, Ding Fang-yu
Original abstract (English)
Chikungunya is undergoing global expansion, but how El Niño–Southern Oscillation (ENSO) influences its transmission remains unclear. We aim to assess the impact of ENSO phases on chikungunya incidence via temperature and precipitation teleconnections and to project future risk burden under climate change. We compiled annual national and subnational chikungunya case data (2008–2024), quantified ENSO–climate teleconnections using the E-index (eastern Pacific El Niño) and C-index (central Pacific La Niña), and applied distributed-lag time-series models to assess the teleconnection-mediated impact of ENSO on chikungunya incidence. We further projected future risk burden under climate change scenarios. Modelling shows that El Niño increased chikungunya risk after a 2-year lag (cumulative relative risk [CRR] = 1.40, 95% confidence interval [CI]: 1.02–1.85), while La Niña suppressed it (CRR = 0.09, 95% CI: 0.07–0.13). Temperature teleconnections were the dominant modifier of spatial heterogeneity in effects. Under all scenarios, El Niño-driven warming led to positive median excess cases, the highest under SSP2-4.5, whereas La Niña-driven changes projected smaller, highly uncertain reductions. ENSO influences chikungunya transmission through asymmetric and spatially heterogeneous teleconnection pathways. Integrating ENSO forecasts into surveillance efforts has the potential to enhance preparedness in climate-sensitive regions.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Chikungunya is undergoing global expansion, but how El Niño–Southern Oscillation (ENSO) influences its transmission remains unclear.
Design and population
We aim to assess the impact of ENSO phases on chikungunya incidence via temperature and precipitation teleconnections and to project future risk burden under climate change.
Core methods
We compiled annual national and subnational chikungunya case data (2008–2024), quantified ENSO–climate teleconnections using the E-index (eastern Pacific El Niño) and C-index (central Pacific La Niña), and applied distributed-lag time-series models to assess the teleconnection-mediated impact of ENSO on chikungunya incidence.
Main results
Modelling shows that El Niño increased chikungunya risk after a 2-year lag (cumulative relative risk [CRR] = 1.40, 95% confidence interval [CI]: 1.02–1.85), while La Niña suppressed it (CRR = 0.09, 95% CI: 0.07–0.13).
Interpretation and novelty
ENSO influences chikungunya transmission through asymmetric and spatially heterogeneous teleconnection pathways.
Scientific and public-health significance
Integrating ENSO forecasts into surveillance efforts has the potential to enhance preparedness in climate-sensitive regions.
Limitations and evidence strength
Temperature teleconnections were the dominant modifier of spatial heterogeneity in effects.

2024–2025年留尼汪岛暴发疫情中两名狼疮患者出现基孔肯雅病毒再感染的证据

Evidence of chikungunya virus reinfection in two patients with lupus during the 2024–2025 outbreak on Reunion Island
作者： Anissa Desmoulin, Dimitri Kornblum, Marie-Christine Jaffar-Bandjee, Etienne Frumence, Aurélie Foucher, Patrice Poubeau, Johan Benhard, Patrick Gérardin, Emilie Mosnier, Marie‐Christine Jaffar‐Bandjee
摘要中文翻译摘要 基孔肯雅病毒感染通常被认为可赋予长期免疫力。在2024–2025年留尼汪岛暴发疫情期间,我们在系统性红斑狼疮患者中确认了一例再感染病例及一例疑似再感染病例。这些发现挑战了这一传统认知,揭示了复杂的发病机制,并表明再感染可能发生,尤其是在特定人群中。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
基孔肯雅病毒感染曾被认为可赋予长期免疫力,但尚不清楚是否会出现再感染,尤其是在系统性红斑狼疮患者中。本研究旨在确定2024–2025年留尼汪岛暴发疫情期间是否观察到再感染现象。
设计与对象
本研究于2024–2025年留尼汪岛暴发疫情期间开展,研究对象为系统性红斑狼疮患者。
核心方法
所提供的证据未报告任何用于识别或确认再感染的方法细节(A1)。
主要结果
在2024–2025年留尼汪岛暴发疫情期间,两名系统性红斑狼疮患者被确认及疑似基孔肯雅病毒再感染。
解释与创新
作者将研究结果解释为对长期免疫力范式的挑战,并提出一种允许基孔肯雅病毒再感染的复杂发病机制,尤其是在系统性红斑狼疮患者中。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,系统性红斑狼疮患者可能出现基孔肯雅病毒再感染,提示需加强抗病毒药物治疗,并在诊断和管理中考虑再感染的可能性。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据未报告任何局限性或证据强度评估(A3)。

Evidence of chikungunya virus reinfection in two patients with lupus during the 2024–2025 outbreak on Reunion Island

Authors: Anissa Desmoulin, Dimitri Kornblum, Marie-Christine Jaffar-Bandjee, Etienne Frumence, Aurélie Foucher, Patrice Poubeau, Johan Benhard, Patrick Gérardin, Emilie Mosnier, Marie‐Christine Jaffar‐Bandjee
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Chikungunya virus infection is considered to confer prolonged immunity. During the 2024–2025 outbreak in Reunion Island, we identified a confirmed and a probable reinfection in patients with systemic lupus erythematosus. These findings challenge this paradigm, underly a complex pathophysiology, and suggest that reinfection may occur, particularly in specific populations.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Chikungunya virus infection was thought to confer prolonged immunity, yet it was unclear whether reinfection could occur, especially in patients with systemic lupus erythematosus. The study aimed to determine if reinfection was observed during the 2024–2025 outbreak on Reunion Island.
Design and population
The study was performed during the 2024–2025 outbreak in Reunion Island and focused on patients with systemic lupus erythematosus.
Core methods
The supplied evidence does not report any details about the methods used to identify or confirm reinfection (A1).
Main results
Two patients with systemic lupus erythematosus were identified with confirmed and probable chikungunya virus reinfection during the 2024–2025 outbreak on Reunion Island.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as challenging the paradigm of prolonged immunity and propose a complex pathophysiology that allows chikungunya virus reinfection, particularly in patients with systemic lupus erythematosus.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings suggest that chikungunya virus reinfection can occur in patients with systemic lupus erythematosus, indicating a need for enhanced surveillance and consideration of reinfection in diagnosis and management.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report any limitations or provide an assessment of evidence strength (A3).

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

巴西萨尔瓦多市紧急护理单元登革热等虫媒病毒疾病共循环现象及城市环境中奥罗普奇病毒检测研究

Surveillance of arboviral diseases in an emergency care unit in Salvador, Bahia: persistence of co-circulation of Dengue and Chikungunya viruses and detection of Oropouche virus in an urban context
作者： ALB. Pinheiro
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在解决热带和亚热带地区虫媒病毒性疾病的公共卫生问题,特别关注登革热和基孔肯雅病毒病毒的共循环现象以及城市环境中奥罗普奇病毒的检测。背景强调了这些疾病的重要性,因为它们与促进媒介病毒繁殖的气候条件有关。
设计与对象
本研究于2023年4月至2025年3月期间在巴西萨尔瓦多市UPA Santo Antônio紧急护理单元进行,纳入了具有虫媒病毒症状且同意参与的个体。
核心方法
从参与者处收集了社会人口学、临床数据和血清样本,并通过ELISA和RT-PCR检测病毒的存在。
主要结果
共纳入370名参与者,病例主要集中在研究初期。在345份检测样本中,120份(34.8%)呈阳性,其中登革热最为常见(23%),其次是基孔肯雅热(10.7%),奥罗普奇病毒在3.4%的样本中被检测到。
解释与创新
本研究将登革热和基孔肯雅病毒病毒的共循环视为临床诊断的挑战,强调了需要更敏感的实验室监测。奥罗普奇病毒在亚马逊地区以外的检测被指出为一项新发现,表明其地理分布正在扩展。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果突显了整合监测在虫媒病毒早期检测和监控中的重要性,特别是在城市环境中,以支持针对易感人群的针对性控制策略和公共卫生政策。
局限与证据强度
本研究的横断面设计限制了因果推论的能力,且病例纳入依赖于自述症状。证据强度中等,因为研究仅基于一个紧急护理单元,未能反映更广泛的人群趋势。

Surveillance of arboviral diseases in an emergency care unit in Salvador, Bahia: persistence of co-circulation of Dengue and Chikungunya viruses and detection of Oropouche virus in an urban context

Authors: ALB. Pinheiro
Original abstract (English)
INTRODUÇÃO: As arboviroses são doenças infecciosas relevantes em regiões tropicais e subtropicais, favorecidas pelo clima quente e úmido que contribui para a proliferação de vetores, especialmente mosquitos do gênero Aedes. No Brasil, destacam-se os vírus da dengue (DENV), chikungunya (CHIKV), Zika (ZIKV) e oropouche (OROV), com aumento expressivo de casos e expansão geográfica. Em 2025, foram notificados 1.657.377 casos prováveis de dengue, 4.153 de Zika, 129.480 de chikungunya e 11.987 de Oropouche (BRASIL, 2025), evidenciando a relevância dessas doenças como problema de saúde pública. OBJETIVOS: Realizar vigilância clínica e molecular de casos suspeitos de arboviroses em uma unidade de pronto atendimento em Salvador, Bahia, analisando a circulação viral e sua relação com fatores clínicos e sociodemográficos. METODOLOGIA: Estudo transversal, realizado entre abril de 2023 e março de 2025, na UPA Santo Antônio, Salvador-BA. Incluíram-se indivíduos com sintomas compatíveis com arboviroses, mediante consentimento. Foram coletados dados sociodemográficos, clínicos e amostras de soro, analisadas por ELISA e RT-PCR. RESULTADOS: Foram incluídos 370 participantes, com maior incidência no primeiro semestre dos anos. Quanto à raça/cor, 38,8% se autodeclararam negros, 48,7% pardos e 13,0% brancos. Os sintomas mais frequentes foram febres (97,4%), cefaleia (94,1%), mialgia (89,4%) e artralgia (81,5%). Das 345 amostras testadas, 120 (34,8%) foram positivas. Dengue foi a mais prevalente (23%), seguida por chikungunya (10,7%). O ZIKV apresentou baixa detecção (1,4%). O OROV foi identificado em 3,4% das amostras por RT-PCR. DISCUSSÃO: A detecção de OROV fora da região Amazônica reforça sua expansão geográfica e possível adaptação a vetores urbanos. A co-circulação de arbovírus dificulta o diagnóstico clínico e exige maior sensibilidade da vigilância laboratorial. Fatores ambientais, como chuvas e urbanização, contribuem para a manutenção da transmissão. CONCLUSÃO: Os achados destacam a importância da vigilância integrada para detecção precoce e monitoramento das arboviroses, subsidiando estratégias de controle e políticas públicas voltadas a populações vulneráveis.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the public health problem of arboviral diseases in tropical and subtropical regions, particularly focusing on the co-circulation of Dengue and Chikungunya viruses and the detection of Oropouche virus in an urban setting. The background highlights the relevance of these diseases due to their association with climate conditions that favor vector proliferation.
Design and population
A cross-sectional study was conducted between April 2023 and March 2025 in the UPA Santo Antônio emergency care unit in Salvador, Bahia, involving individuals with symptoms compatible with arboviroses who provided consent for participation.
Core methods
Sociodemographic, clinical data, and serum samples were collected from participants. The samples were analyzed using ELISA and RT-PCR to detect viral presence.
Main results
A total of 370 participants were included, with the highest incidence in the first half of the study period. Of the 345 tested samples, 120 (34.8%) were positive, with Dengue being the most prevalent (23%), followed by Chikungunya (10.7%), and Oropouche virus detected in 3.4% of the samples.
Interpretation and novelty
The study interprets the co-circulation of Dengue and Chikungunya viruses as a challenge for clinical diagnosis, emphasizing the need for more sensitive laboratory surveillance. The detection of Oropouche virus outside the Amazon region is noted as a novel finding indicating its geographic expansion.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings underscore the importance of integrated surveillance for early detection and monitoring of arboviroses, particularly in urban areas, to support targeted control strategies and public health policies for vulnerable populations.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design, which does not allow for causal inference, and the reliance on self-reported symptoms for case inclusion. The evidence is moderate, as it is based on a single emergency care unit and does not account for broader population trends.

Chikungunya-Associated Acute Respiratory Distress Syndrome: A Rare Fatal Case
作者： Arkaprava Singharoy, Payel Bose, Tanmay Banerjee, Sagnik Das, Prerona Bhowmick

问题与背景
本研究旨在探究基孔肯雅病毒与急性呼吸窘迫综合征的关联及其潜在致死作用。
设计与对象
本病例报告描述了一项在研究期间观察到的单例基孔肯雅热相关急性呼吸窘迫综合征罕见致死病例。

The study aimed to investigate the association of chikungunya virus with acute respiratory distress syndrome and its potential role in causing fatal outcomes.

稳定、无血清悬浮培养HEK293T细胞系用于持续性基孔肯雅病毒样颗粒生产

Development of stable, serum-free suspension HEK293T cells for continuous Chikungunya VLPs production
作者： Abhilasha K. Rani, Wajihul Hasan Khan, Chandra Shekhar Kumar, Manidipa Banerjee, Anurag S. Rathore
摘要中文翻译通过温度转换,在悬浮适应性培养中实现了5倍产量提升,产量通过基于吸光度的方法进行定量。所建立的HEK293T细胞系能够高效分泌基孔肯雅病毒样颗粒(CHIK-VLPs),为病毒样颗粒生产提供了一种通用且可扩展的方法,且不依赖抗生素或胎牛血清(FBS)。摘要基孔肯雅病毒(基孔肯雅病毒)感染已在100多个国家发生。病毒样颗粒(VLPs)在结构上复制了天然病毒,但不含任何感染性基因组,有望作为预防性疫苗。然而,采用传统HEK293T贴壁细胞培养生产CHIK-VLPs(依赖胎牛血清)面临可扩展性限制。为解决这一挑战,本研究建立了能够生产CHIK-VLPs的稳定HEK293T细胞系,并进一步将其适应至无血清悬浮培养,在提高可扩展性的同时降低了与FBS相关的污染风险。此外,在培养过程中从37°C向30°C的温度转换有助于提高产物产量。为确认VLPs表达,采用了多种分析技术,包括电子显微镜,用于评估稳定贴壁及悬浮适应HEK293T细胞所产VLPs的形态。该工艺为生物制药行业提供了有价值的贡献,为大规模VLPs基础疫苗开发提供了潜在可能。致谢作者感谢Sanghati Bhattacharya(印度德里IIT)对CHIK-VLPs完整质量肽图分析的质谱分析。我们诚挚感谢实验室同事及工作人员对本项目的协助。伦理声明利益冲突 作者声明无利益冲突。补充信息Pu...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在解决传统HEK293T贴壁细胞培养生产基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV)病毒样颗粒(VLPs)的可扩展性限制。VLPs作为基孔肯雅热疫苗开发的有前景候选物,因依赖胎牛血清(FBS)而面临挑战。
设计与对象
本研究建立并适应了一个稳定的HEK293T细胞系,用于无血清悬浮培养以生产基孔肯雅病毒VLPs。该工艺在实验室环境中进行,细胞系作为VLP生产的宿主。
核心方法
研究人员在悬浮培养过程中采用了从37°C向30°C的温度转换以提高VLP产量。同时,采用电子显微镜评估了稳定贴壁及悬浮适应HEK293T细胞所产VLPs的形态。
主要结果
通过温度转换,悬浮适应性培养中的VLP产量提升了5倍,产量通过基于吸光度的方法进行定量。电子显微镜证实了VLPs的结构完整性。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,适应后的HEK293T细胞系能够在无血清悬浮条件下高效生产基孔肯雅病毒VLPs,为基孔肯雅热疫苗开发提供了一种可扩展且无污染的方法。
科研与公卫意义
该方法为基孔肯雅病毒VLPs生产提供了通用且可扩展的途径,有助于基孔肯雅热疫苗的开发,并可能提升对基孔肯雅热暴发的公共卫生应对能力。
局限与证据强度
本研究仅限于实验室观察,未纳入临床或流行病学数据。该方法在技术可行性方面证据充分,但在真实世界环境中的更广泛验证尚缺。

Development of stable, serum-free suspension HEK293T cells for continuous Chikungunya VLPs production

Authors: Abhilasha K. Rani, Wajihul Hasan Khan, Chandra Shekhar Kumar, Manidipa Banerjee, Anurag S. Rathore
Original abstract (English)
A 5-fold enhancement was achieved in suspension-adapted culture using a temperature shift, quantified by absorbance-based method. The established HEK293T cell line was capable of efficient CHIK-VLPs secretion provides a versatile and scalable method for VLPs production, it is also independent of antibiotics and FBS. Abstract Chikungunya Virus (CHIKV) infections occur in over 100 countries. Virus-like particles (VLPs), which replicate the structure of native virus without containing any infectious genome, hold promise as prophylactic vaccines. However, production of CHIK-VLPs using traditional HEK293T adherent cell culture, which relies on use of fetal bovine serum (FBS), faces scalability limitations. To address this challenge, we established a stable HEK293T cell line capable of producing CHIK-VLPs and further adapted it to suspension culture under serum-free conditions, improving scalability while reducing the risk of contamination associated with FBS. Further, a temperature shift during the process from 37 °C to 30 °C was incorporated to enhance the product yield. To confirm VLPs expression, various analytical techniques were employed, including electron microscopy, which assessed the morphology of VLPs produced by both stable adherent and suspension-adapted HEK293T cells. This process represents a valuable contribution to the biopharmaceutical industry, offering potential for large-scale VLPs-based vaccine development. Acknowledgements The authors would like to thank Sanghati Bhattacharya (IIT-Delhi, India) for the MS analysis of Intact mass peptide mapping of CHIK-VLPs. We sincerely thank our lab colleagues and staff who helped us to complete this project. Ethics declarations Competing interests The authors declare no competing interests. Additional information Pu...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the scalability limitations of traditional HEK293T adherent cell culture for producing Chikungunya Virus (CHIKV) Virus-like Particles (VLPs), which are promising for vaccine development but face challenges due to reliance on fetal bovine serum (FBS).
Design and population
The study involved establishing and adapting a stable HEK293T cell line for serum-free suspension culture to produce CHIKV VLPs. The process was conducted in a laboratory setting, with the cell line serving as the host for VLP production.
Core methods
The researchers used a temperature shift from 37 °C to 30 °C during the suspension culture process to enhance VLP yield. They also employed electron microscopy to assess the morphology of VLPs produced by both stable adherent and suspension-adapted HEK293T cells.
Main results
A 5-fold enhancement in VLP production was achieved in the suspension-adapted culture using the temperature shift, as quantified by an absorbance-based method. Electron microscopy confirmed the structural integrity of the VLPs.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate that the adapted HEK293T cell line can produce CHIKV VLPs efficiently under serum-free suspension conditions, offering a scalable and contamination-free method for vaccine development.
Scientific and public-health significance
This method provides a versatile and scalable approach for producing CHIKV VLPs, which could support the development of vaccines against chikungunya infections and potentially improve public health preparedness for outbreaks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to laboratory-based observations and does not include clinical or epidemiological data. The evidence is strong for the technical feasibility of the method but lacks broader validation in real-world settings.

伊朗东南部登革热媒介埃及伊蚊的起源与系统地理学

Emergence and phylogeography of the dengue vector Aedes aegypti in Southeastern Iran.
作者： Jalil Nejati, Mona Koosha, Nayyereh Choubdar, Sara Rahimy, Hassan Balouch, Mohammad Ali Oshaghi
摘要中文翻译背景:埃及伊蚊(Aedes (Stegomyia) aegypti (Linnaeus))是登革热、基孔肯雅热、寨卡热和黄热病病毒的主要媒介。其近期在伊朗东南部的检测引发公共卫生关切,担忧新发虫媒病毒向该地区扩散。本研究首次对伊朗锡斯坦与俾路支省(SBP)埃及伊蚊种群进行遗传与系统地理学分析,以推断其起源与入侵路径。方法:对伊朗锡斯坦与俾路支省边境地区、港口及城市新采集的埃及伊蚊标本进行线粒体COI与ND4基因测序分析。使用PopART软件中的TCS法构建单倍型网络分析,并利用全球参考序列进行系统发育分析。结果:伊朗标本包含7个COI单倍型(n=18)与10个ND4单 haplotypes(n=17)。COI系统发育分析将伊朗标本分入两个主要分支,而ND4分析则将其分布于多个衍生分支,主要与拉丁美洲(巴西、墨西哥)或非洲的谱系聚类。一份伊朗标本与沙特阿拉伯序列(自展值:98%)在基部区域显示密切关系。COI+ND4联合分析显示伊朗标本与斯里兰卡标本形成单系分支,与其他全球谱系相区别。全球COI网络(n=47)呈星形拓扑结构,单倍型1为优势单倍型,在10份伊朗标本中共享。ND4网络(n=31)显示复杂拓扑结构,包含18个单倍型,其中沙特阿拉伯与一份伊朗标本(~30个突变步骤)可能代表外围根源。结论:检测到多样化埃及伊蚊分支,证实该媒介已在伊朗东南部定殖。结果支持多重引入与遗传连接性,涉及拉丁美洲、非洲与南亚,指向新兴入侵走廊。亟需持续媒介监测与综合病媒控制,以指导预防策略。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在调查埃及伊蚊——登革热、基孔肯雅热、寨卡热与黄热病病毒的主要媒介——在伊朗东南部的起源与入侵路径。
设计与对象
本研究聚焦于2021年至2023年间在伊朗锡斯坦与俾路支省(SBP)边境地区、港口及城市采集的埃及伊蚊标本。
核心方法
对新采集的埃及伊蚊标本进行线粒体COI与ND4基因测序分析。使用PopART软件中的TCS法构建单倍型网络分析,并利用全球参考序列进行系统发育分析。
主要结果
伊朗标本包含7个COI单倍型(n=18)与10个ND4单倍型(n=17)。COI系统发育分析将伊朗标本分入两个主要分支,而ND4分析则将其分布于多个衍生分支,主要与拉丁美洲(巴西、墨西哥)或非洲的谱系聚类。一份伊朗标本与沙特阿拉伯序列(自展值:98%)在基部区域显示密切关系。COI+ND4联合分析显示伊朗标本与斯里兰卡标本形成单系分支,与其他全球谱系相区别。全球COI网络(n=47)呈星形拓扑结构,单倍型1为优势单倍型,在10份伊朗标本中共享。
解释与创新
研究结果证实埃及伊蚊已在伊朗东南部定殖,并提示多重引入与遗传连接性,涉及拉丁美洲、非洲与南亚,表明存在新兴入侵走廊。
科研与公卫意义
结果凸显持续媒介监测与综合病媒控制的必要性,以指导该地区虫媒病毒传播的预防策略。
局限与证据强度
本研究基于埃及伊蚊标本的遗传分析,可能无法全面反映生态或流行病学因素。证据强度为中等,因其依赖系统发育与单倍型网络分析,而缺乏直接病毒学或临床数据。

Emergence and phylogeography of the dengue vector Aedes aegypti in Southeastern Iran.

Authors: Jalil Nejati, Mona Koosha, Nayyereh Choubdar, Sara Rahimy, Hassan Balouch, Mohammad Ali Oshaghi
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Aedes (Stegomyia) aegypti (Linnaeus) is the primary vector of dengue, chikungunya, Zika, and yellow fever viruses. Its recent detection in southeastern Iran raises public health concerns about arbovirus spread to new regions. This study provides the first genetic and phylogeographic analysis of Ae. aegypti populations from Sistan and Baluchistan Province (SBP), Iran, to infer their origin and invasion pathways. METHODS: Mitochondrial COI and ND4 genes were analysed in newly collected Ae. aegypti specimens from border areas, ports, and urban centres of SBP. Haplotype network analyses were constructed using the TCS method in PopART, and phylogenetic analyses were conducted using global reference sequences. RESULTS: Iranian specimens comprised 7 COI haplotypes (n = 18) and 10 ND4 haplotypes (n = 17). COI phylogeny placed Iranian specimens into two main clades, while ND4 analysis distributed them across several derived clades, mostly clustering with lineages from Latin America (Brazil, Mexico) or Africa. One Iranian specimen showed a close relationship with a Saudi Arabian sequence (bootstrap: 98%) near the basal region. Combined COI + ND4 analysis revealed a monophyletic clade of Iranian specimens with a Sri Lankan specimen, distinct from other global lineages. The global COI network (n = 47) showed a star-like topology with a dominant haplotype 1 shared among 10 Iranian specimens. The ND4 network (n = 31) revealed a complex topology with 18 haplotypes, where a Saudi Arabian and one Iranian specimen (~30 mutational steps) possibly represented the peripheral root. CONCLUSIONS: Detection of diverse Ae. aegypti clades confirm establishment of this vector in southeastern Iran. Results support multiple introductions and genetic connectivity with Latin America, Africa, and South Asia, pointing to an emerging invasion corridor. Continued genomic surveillance and integrated vector monitoring are urgently needed to guide prevention strategies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to investigate the origin and invasion pathways of Aedes aegypti, the primary vector of dengue, chikungunya, Zika, and yellow fever viruses, in southeastern Iran.
Design and population
The study focused on Ae. aegypti specimens collected from border areas, ports, and urban centres of Sistan and Baluchistan Province (SBP), Iran, between 2021 and 2023.
Core methods
Mitochondrial COI and ND4 genes were sequenced and analysed in newly collected Ae. Haplotype network analyses were constructed using the TCS method in PopART, and phylogenetic analyses were conducted using global reference sequences.
Main results
Iranian specimens comprised 7 COI haplotypes (n = 18) and 10 ND4 haplotypes (n = 17). COI phylogeny placed Iranian specimens into two main clades, while ND4 analysis distributed them across several derived clades, mostly clustering with lineages from Latin America (Brazil, Mexico) or Africa. One Iranian specimen showed a close relationship with a Saudi Arabian sequence (bootstrap: 98%) near the basal region. Combined COI + ND4 analysis revealed a monophyletic clade of Iranian specimens with a Sri Lankan specimen, distinct from other global lineages. The global COI network (n = 47) showed a star-like topology with a dominant haplotype 1 shared among 10 Iranian specimens.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings confirm the establishment of Ae. aegypti in southeastern Iran and suggest multiple introductions and genetic connectivity with Latin America, Africa, and South Asia, indicating an emerging invasion corridor.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results highlight the need for continued genomic surveillance and integrated vector monitoring to guide prevention strategies against the spread of arboviruses in the region.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on genetic analysis of Ae. aegypti specimens, which may not fully capture ecological or epidemiological factors. The evidence is moderate as it relies on phylogenetic and haplotype network analyses without direct virological or clinical data.

指动式微流控设备用于即时检测循环无细胞核酸提取

Finger-actuated microfluidic device for point-of-care circulating cell-free nucleic acid extraction.
作者： Ghulam Musaddaq, Labibah Ehsan, Yano Yoga, Joshua Castillo, Linda Marriott, Nicholas R. Leslie, Deborah A. Williamson, Thomas Tran, Chuan Kok Lim, Maïwenn Kersaudy-Kerhoas
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究针对即时检测点快速、可及核酸提取的关键需求,以支持分散式分子诊断,特别是针对临床样本中低丰度循环无细胞核酸。
设计与对象
本研究展示了一种完全指动式微流控设备,设计用于无需电源、无需移液器的核酸提取,通过手动操作完成裂解、结合、洗涤与洗脱的“样本-洗脱液”工作流程。
核心方法
原型设备采用DNA加标水样与人源混合血浆进行评估,其性能与商用磁珠法手动台式工作流程及自动化机器人工作流程进行了基准测试,并优化磁珠体积以实现最大回收率。
主要结果
在DNA加标水样(5-20 ng mL−1)中,该设备的回收率为84.6%,而台式对照为90.7%;在人源混合血浆样本中,初始样本体积为100、75和50 μL时,该设备的回收率分别比台式对照提高18%、62%和552%。
解释与创新
作者将该设备解读为可实现快速、无仪器依赖的提取工作流程,其性能与标准方案相当,展现出在资源有限环境下支持分散式诊断的潜力。
科研与公卫意义
该设备有助于通过支持分散式核酸提取来促进传染病的及时分子检测,特别是在实验室基础设施有限的地区,从而改善诊断的可及性。
局限与证据强度
该证据仅限于概念验证和试点测试,使用非特异性核酸与混合病毒靶标,缺乏针对基孔肯雅病毒的详细临床验证,限制了结果的普遍性与推理强度。

Finger-actuated microfluidic device for point-of-care circulating cell-free nucleic acid extraction.

Authors: Ghulam Musaddaq, Labibah Ehsan, Yano Yoga, Joshua Castillo, Linda Marriott, Nicholas R. Leslie, Deborah A. Williamson, Thomas Tran, Chuan Kok Lim, Maïwenn Kersaudy-Kerhoas
Original abstract (English)
Timely and accurate nucleic acid extraction is a critical bottleneck in point-of-care (POC) molecular diagnostics, particularly for low-abundance cell-free nucleic acids (DNA, RNA, miRNA) in plasma. We present a fully finger-actuated microfluidic device for power-free, pipette-free, nucleic acid extraction. The prototype device integrates finger-actuated valves, pump and blister reagent storage, enabling a complete, intuitive, "sample-to-eluate" workflow, including lysis, binding, washing and elution via manual actuation in approximately 35 minutes. The device operation was benchmarked against a commercial magnetic bead-based manual benchtop and an automated robotic workflow. In DNA-spiked aqueous samples (5-20 ng mL -1 ), the device demonstrated recoveries of 84.6%, against 90.7% on the bench. Optimisation of magnetic bead volume identified 50 μL as the threshold for maximal recovery while minimising reagent consumption. Recovery from human pooled plasma samples were found to be on average 18, 62, and 552% higher than that obtained with the control bench extraction on initial sample volumes of 100, 75 and 50 μL, respectively. Coefficient of variation (CV%) for all techniques remained within reported laboratory ranges (20-80%). The device's clinical utility was piloted using patient samples positive for cytomegalovirus (CMV), monkeypox virus (MPXV) or chikungunya (CHIKV). The device achieved successful qPCR amplification for all targets, with viral DNA Cq values within as little as 0.3 cycles of the standard protocol. This instrument-free, manual circulating nucleic acid extraction tool could help bridge the gap between lateral flow based nucleic acid amplification technologies and real-world decentralised diagnostics, offering a scalable solution for infectious disease management in resource-limited settings.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the critical need for rapid, accessible nucleic acid extraction at the point of care to enable decentralized molecular diagnostics, particularly for low-abundance circulating cell-free nucleic acids in clinical samples.
Design and population
The research presents a fully finger-actuated microfluidic device designed for power-free, pipette-free nucleic acid extraction, integrating manual actuation for lysis, binding, washing, and elution in a sample-to-eluate workflow.
Core methods
The prototype device was evaluated using DNA-spiked aqueous samples and human pooled plasma, with performance benchmarked against commercial magnetic bead-based manual benchtop and automated robotic workflows, including optimization of magnetic bead volume for maximal recovery.
Main results
In DNA-spiked aqueous samples (5-20 ng mL−1), the device achieved recoveries of 84.6% compared to 90.7% for the bench workflow, while recovery from human pooled plasma was 18%, 62%, and 552% higher than the bench extraction at initial sample volumes of 100, 75, and 50 μL, respectively.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the device as enabling a rapid, instrument-free extraction workflow that maintains comparable performance to standard protocols, demonstrating potential for decentralized diagnostics in resource-limited settings.
Scientific and public-health significance
The device could facilitate timely molecular testing for infectious diseases by supporting decentralized nucleic acid extraction, particularly where laboratory infrastructure is limited, thereby improving access to diagnostics.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited to proof-of-concept and pilot testing with non-specific nucleic acids and mixed viral targets, lacking detailed clinical validation for chikungunya virus specifically, which constrains generalizability and strength of inference.
