用于控制白纹伊蚊的环保型柠檬醛纳米乳剂:整合分子对接的多靶点毒理学评估机制

Eco-safe citral nanoemulsion for Aedes albopictus control: Mechanistic multi-target toxicological evaluation integrating molecular docking.
作者： Souvik Bag, Surajit Ghosh, Dibyendu Saha, Antara Mondal, Nimai Chandra Saha, Soumendranath Chatterjee, Paolo Pastorino, Shubhajit Saha
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
由白纹伊蚊传播的登革热、基孔肯雅热、寨卡病毒病和黄热病等蚊媒疾病的广泛流行,迫切需要环保且可持续的媒介控制方案。
设计与对象
本研究通过低能乳化法以吐温80为乳化剂,开发并表征了一种基于柠檬醛的纳米乳剂,旨在提高杀幼虫效果并保障环境安全。
核心方法
对三龄白纹伊蚊(Aedes albopictus)进行杀幼虫生物测定,以评估柠檬醛纳米乳剂与未制剂化柠檬醛的毒力差异。
主要结果
对第三龄埃及伊蚊的幼虫毒力测定显示,柠檬醛纳米乳剂(LC 50:18.886 mg/L;LC 90:78.209 mg/L)的杀虫效果显著高于未制剂化柠檬醛(LC 50:25.777 mg/L;LC 90:99.481 mg/L)。
解释与创新
形态学研究与扫描电镜分析显示,纳米乳剂处理的幼虫存在严重结构损伤,包括表皮塌陷、气门变形和肛鳃畸形。
科研与公卫意义
生化测定显示过氧化氢酶(CAT)、超氧化物歧化酶(SOD)和丙二醛(MDA)水平升高,提示氧化应激诱导。
局限与证据强度
使用摇蚊幼虫进行的环境安全性评估显示急性毒性较低(52.136 mg/L下<10%固定化),表明对靶标物种具有选择性作用。

Eco-safe citral nanoemulsion for Aedes albopictus control: Mechanistic multi-target toxicological evaluation integrating molecular docking.

Authors: Souvik Bag, Surajit Ghosh, Dibyendu Saha, Antara Mondal, Nimai Chandra Saha, Soumendranath Chatterjee, Paolo Pastorino, Shubhajit Saha
Original abstract (English)
The widespread transmission of mosquito-borne diseases such as dengue, chikungunya, Zika, and yellow fever by Aedes albopictus necessitates environmentally sustainable vector control solutions. This study developed and characterized a citral-based nanoemulsion using Tween 80 via low-energy emulsification, aiming to enhance larvicidal efficacy and environmental safety. Larvicidal bioassays against third instar A. albopictus revealed significantly higher potency for citral nanoemulsion (LC 50 : 18.886 mg/L; LC 90 : 78.209 mg/L) compared to unformulated citral (LC 50 : 25.777 mg/L; LC 90 : 99.481 mg/L). Morphological study and SEM analysis demonstrated severe structural damage in nanoemulsion-treated larvae, including cuticle collapse, spiracle deformation, and anal gill disfigurement. Biochemical assays indicated elevated catalase (CAT), superoxide dismutase (SOD) and malondialdehyde (MDA) levels, suggesting oxidative stress induction. Molecular docking revealed moderate to high binding affinities of citral to CAT (-6.3 kcal/mol), SOD (-4.5 kcal/mol), acetylcholine receptor (AChR; -6.0 kcal/mol), and juvenile hormone-binding protein (JHBP; -7.0 kcal/mol), involving hydrogen bonding and hydrophobic interactions. These interactions imply multi-target disruption of antioxidant defence, neural signalling, and endocrine regulation, contributing to larval mortality. Environmental safety evaluation using Chironomus larvae showed low acute toxicity (<10% immobilization at 52.136 mg/L), indicating selective action toward target species. Collectively, the findings highlight citral nanoemulsion as a potent, biodegradable and target-specific larvicide with minimal non-target effects, aligning with sustainable mosquito control strategies. Future work should focus on field validation, adult mosquito susceptibility, and chronic ecological safety to facilitate large-scale application.

安第斯-亚马孙走廊蚊媒病毒的再次出现:哥伦比亚、厄瓜多尔和委内瑞拉的生态流行病学动态及基因组监测

Reemergence of arboviruses in the Andean-Amazonian corridor: eco-epidemiological dynamics and surveillance in Colombia, Ecuador, and Venezuela
作者： Yenddy N. Carrero, Jesús A. Mosquera
摘要中文翻译再次出现的传染病对全球公共卫生构成日益严重的威胁,尤其是在热带地区,环境变化、快速城市化、疫苗接种覆盖率下降以及人类流动促进了多种病原体的传播。拉丁美洲目前正面临前所未有的流行病学危机,传染病在不平等和碎片化卫生系统的背景下传播。安第斯-亚马孙走廊是多种人兽共患病媒介传播疾病的流行病学热点地区。本文旨在分析2023年至2026年上述国家再次出现疾病的流行病学趋势,识别与其再次出现相关的生态、社会及健康因素。研究结果显示,虫媒病毒(如登革热、基孔肯雅热和黄热病)持续上升,亚马孙地区疟疾亦持续存在。在厄瓜多尔,2025年报告37,840例登革热病例,死亡65例;在哥伦比亚,2024年至2025年记录黄热病疫情,确诊人类病例132例。委内瑞拉2025年至2026年的黄热病疫情显示出再次出现的迹象,主要与该病毒在野生循环中的传播及向邻近地区扩散的风险相关。研究发现,气候变化、森林砍伐、迁移、流行病学监测薄弱、媒介控制不力、有限的卫生服务获取、信息疫情、健康教育不足以及疫苗接种犹豫等因素共同导致了疾病的再次出现。研究结论指出,有必要在拉丁美洲实施‘一个健康’方法,其必要性不仅基于报告病例数量,更在于未被检测和未报告病例的规模,以及基因组监测以识别变异、追踪传播、监测药物耐药性并预测疫情,从而转变对复杂、新发且常被忽视疾病的认知。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在分析2023年至2026年哥伦比亚、厄瓜多尔和委内瑞拉再次出现疾病的流行病学趋势,识别与其再次出现相关的生态、社会及健康因素。
设计与对象
本研究聚焦安第斯-亚马孙走廊这一多种人兽共患病媒介传播疾病的流行病学热点地区,并考察上述国家虫媒病毒(如登革热、基孔肯雅热和黄热病)的再次出现情况。
核心方法
本研究结合流行病学数据分析、生态建模及基因组监测,追踪虫媒病毒的传播并识别导致其再次出现的因素。
主要结果
在厄瓜多尔,2025年报告37,840例登革热病例,死亡65例;在哥伦比亚,2024年至2025年记录黄热病疫情,确诊人类病例132例。
解释与创新
研究结果强调需采用‘一个健康’方法应对再次出现疾病的复杂动态,并凸显基因组监测及改善公共卫生基础设施的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果凸显加强流行病学监测、媒介控制及健康教育以减轻虫媒病毒传播的重要性,并解决该地区疫苗接种犹豫问题。
局限与证据强度
本研究受限于对报告病例的依赖,可能无法全面反映疾病负担,且缺乏未检测或未报告病例的详细数据。

Reemergence of arboviruses in the Andean-Amazonian corridor: eco-epidemiological dynamics and surveillance in Colombia, Ecuador, and Venezuela

Authors: Yenddy N. Carrero, Jesús A. Mosquera
Original abstract (English)
Re-emerging infectious diseases pose a growing threat to global public health, especially in tropical regions where environmental changes, rapid urbanization, declining vaccination coverage, and human mobility facilitate the transmission of various pathogens. Latin America is currently facing an unprecedented epidemiological crisis, with infectious diseases spreading amid conditions of inequality and fragmented health systems. The Andean-Amazonian corridor represents an epidemiological hotspot for various vector-borne diseases. The objective of this article was to analyze the epidemiological trends of re-emerging diseases in these countries from 2023 to 2026, identifying ecological, social, and health factors linked to their re-emergence. The results reveal a sustained increase in arboviruses such as dengue, chikungunya, and yellow fever, as well as the persistence of malaria in Amazonian areas. In Ecuador, 37,840 cases of dengue were reported in 2025, with 65 deaths, while in Colombia, an outbreak of yellow fever was recorded with 132 confirmed human cases from 2024 to 2025. The situation regarding yellow fever in Venezuela during 2025 and 2026 shows signs of reemergence within the regional context of South America, associated primarily with the circulation of the virus in sylvatic cycles and the risk of spread to neighboring areas. The findings indicate that the interaction between climate change, deforestation, migration, and evident weaknesses in epidemiological surveillance, vector control, limited access to health services, infodemic, poor health education, and vaccine hesitancy contribute to the reemergence. In conclusion, it is necessary to implement a One Health approach in Latin America, which is justified not by the number of reported cases, but by the magnitude of what remains undetected and unreported, as well as genomic surveillance to identify variants, trace transmissions, monitor drug resistance, and anticipate outbreaks, thereby transforming our understanding of complex, emerging, and often neglected diseases.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to analyze the epidemiological trends of re-emerging diseases in Colombia, Ecuador, and Venezuela from 2023 to 2026, identifying ecological, social, and health factors linked to their re-emergence.
Design and population
The study focused on the Andean-Amazonian corridor, which is an epidemiological hotspot for various vector-borne diseases, and examined the re-emergence of arboviruses such as dengue, chikungunya, and yellow fever in these countries.
Core methods
The study used a combination of epidemiological data analysis, ecological modeling, and genomic surveillance to track the spread of arboviruses and identify factors contributing to their re-emergence.
Main results
In Ecuador, 37,840 cases of dengue were reported in 2025, with 65 deaths, while in Colombia, an outbreak of yellow fever was recorded with 132 confirmed human cases from 2024 to 2025.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings highlight the need for a One Health approach to address the complex dynamics of re-emerging diseases, emphasizing the importance of genomic surveillance and improved public health infrastructure.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results underscore the importance of strengthening epidemiological surveillance, vector control, and health education to mitigate the spread of arboviruses and address vaccine hesitancy in the region.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on reported cases, which may not capture the full extent of the disease burden, and the lack of detailed data on undetected or unreported cases.

α病毒非结构蛋白功能与抑制剂的研究

INVESTIGATION OF ALPHAVIRUS NON-STRUCTURAL PROTEIN FUNCTIONS AND INHIBITORS
作者： John Dylan Sears
摘要中文翻译α病毒是一类新兴的蚊媒病毒,可引起慢性关节痛和致命性脑炎。尽管近期有暴发进展,目前尚无抗病毒药物药物可用于治疗任何α病毒感染。因此,开发针对α病毒有效的抗病毒药物药物对全球公共卫生至关重要。α病毒通过其非结构蛋白(nsP)的关键功能合成其基因组,这使其成为理想的抑制剂靶标。我研究了两种蛋白质:nsP2(一种解旋酶和蛋白酶)和nsP3(一种核糖基水解酶),以更好地理解α病毒的复制过程并识别抑制剂。在第2章中,我描述了一种新颖的计算方法,用于识别针对广泛谱系抗病毒药物药物的靶标。该方法将蛋白质折叠软件与其他计算程序结合,以识别nsP2蛋白酶结构域上的保守且可溶性暴露的靶标。在这些位点对基孔肯雅病毒(基孔肯雅病毒)进行突变后,发现了一个位于蛋白酶活性位点对面的靶标,其突变可降低病毒复制。进一步分析表明,该口袋内的分子内相互作用对于遗传多样性广泛的α病毒群体的复制至关重要,当这些相互作用被破坏时,nsP2蛋白酶活性下降,表明这可能是一个新的变构调控位点。由于第2章的研究未能在第3章中发现任何抑制剂,我采用传统的抗病毒药物发现方法,识别并开发了首个针对nsP2解旋酶活性的抑制剂RA-NSP2-1。RA-NSP2-1是一种对映选择性化合物,对α病毒家族具有广泛的活性,并在基孔肯雅病毒的小鼠模型中表现出良好的疗效。我通过基于细胞、酶学和生物物理的实验确定了RA-NSP2-1的作用机制,揭示了抑制其他影响多种疾病RNA解旋酶的方法。我还发现,在α病毒感染过程中,nsP3具有新的功能,即作为RNA核糖基水解酶。我观察到,在基孔肯雅病毒感染后,RNA核糖基化水平升高,而对核糖基水解酶的突变则导致基孔肯雅病毒RNA的核糖基化显著增加,这种现象依赖于PARP12。利用这种升高的RNA核糖基化,我确定了核糖基化RNA被更快降解、翻译效率降低,并引发显著的宿主先天抗病毒药物免疫应答。这些发现为非结构蛋白的功能提供了重要见解,并在α病毒复制研究和抗病毒药物药物开发方面取得了重大进展。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
α病毒是一类新兴的蚊媒病毒,可引起慢性关节痛和致命性脑炎。尽管近期有暴发进展,目前尚无抗病毒药物药物可用于治疗任何α病毒感染。

我研究了两种蛋白质:nsP2(一种解旋酶和蛋白酶)和nsP3(一种核糖基水解酶),以更好地理解α病毒的复制过程并识别抑制剂。

科研与公卫意义
这些发现为非结构蛋白的功能提供了重要见解,并在α病毒复制研究和抗病毒药物药物开发方面取得了重大进展。
局限与证据强度
由于第2章的研究未能在第3章中发现任何抑制剂,我采用传统的抗病毒药物发现方法,识别并开发了首个针对nsP2解旋酶活性的抑制剂RA-NSP2-1。

INVESTIGATION OF ALPHAVIRUS NON-STRUCTURAL PROTEIN FUNCTIONS AND INHIBITORS

Authors: John Dylan Sears
Original abstract (English)
Alphaviruses are an emerging family of mosquito-borne viruses that cause chronic polyarthralgia and lethal encephalitis. Despite recent outbreaks there are currently no antivirals to treat any alphavirus infection. Development of antivirals effective against alphaviruses is therefore important for global public health. Alphaviruses synthesize their genomes via essential functions of their non-structural proteins (nsP), making them ideal targets for inhibitors. I investigated two proteins: nsP2, a helicase and protease, and nsP3, a ribosylhydrolase, to better understand alphavirus replication and identify inhibitors. In chapter 2 I describe a novel computational approach to identify targets for broad spectrum antiviral drugs. This method pairs protein folding software with other computational programs to identify conserved and solvent accessible targets on the nsP2 protease domain. Mutating chikungunya virus (CHIKV) in these sites identified a target opposite from the protease active site that showed reduced replication when mutated. Further analysis identified intramolecular interactions within this pocket are important for replication of a genetically diverse group of alphaviruses and that when they are disrupted nsP2 protease activity decreases indicating that it may be novel allosteric regulatory site. As the study in chapter 2 was unable to identify any inhibitors in chapter 3 I utilized traditional methods of antiviral discovery to identify and develop the first inhibitor of nsP2 helicase activity, RA-NSP2-1. RA-NSP2-1 in an enantioselective compound with broad activity across the alphavirus family that is efficacious in a mouse model of CHIKV. I determined RA-NSP2-1’s mechanism of action using cell-based, enzymatic, and biophysical assays which revealed methods to inhibit other RNA helicases that impact a range of diseases. I also identified a novel function of nsP3 during alphavirus infection as an RNA ribosylhydrolase. I found that RNA ribosylation increases upon CHIKV infection and mutating the ribosylhydrolase led to significant increases in ribosylation of CHIKV RNA in a PARP12 dependent manner. Using this elevated RNA ribosylation I determined that ribosylated RNA is degraded more rapidly, translated less efficiently, and sets of a significant host innate antiviral response. The findings provide important insights into non-structural protein function and are significant advances in the study of alphavirus replication and antiviral development.

气候变化与虫媒病毒性疾病:对监测与基层卫生保健的挑战

Climate Change and Arboviral Diseases: Challenges for Surveillance and Primary Health Care
作者： Maria Terezinha Gomes Pontes, Edylene Maria Dos Santos Pereira, Bruno Ramos Brum, Maria Aparecida de Almeida Araújo, Thaita Thaisi Zago, Sérgio Raimundo Ernesto Machado, Sheila Érika Ferro Ramalho Nobre, Neide Moreira de Souza
摘要中文翻译气候变化已成为二十一世纪最严峻的公共卫生挑战之一,深刻影响着全球传染病的流行病学。在直接受环境变化影响的疾病中,虫媒病毒性疾病(如登革热、寨卡病毒病、基孔肯雅热与黄热病)的传播动态对温度、降水、湿度、城市化及生态系统破坏等变化高度敏感。这些气候与环境变化已扩大了蚊媒媒介的地理分布范围,延长了传播季节,并增加了热带与亚热带地区暴发的频率与强度。在巴西等虫媒病毒已构成显著公共卫生负担的国家,气候变化加剧了既有脆弱性,并为卫生系统带来新的需求,特别是在疾病监测、预防与医疗服务提供方面。本学术论文对气候变化与虫媒病毒性疾病负担日益加重之间的关系进行了批判性分析,强调了流行病学监测系统与基层卫生保健(PHC)所面临的挑战。讨论探索了气候变异与媒介增殖之间的生态机制、卫生服务的组织层面影响、整合式监测的重要性,以及PHC在预防、早期诊断、社区参与与跨部门行动中的战略作用。本文认为,加强气候敏感型卫生治理与韧性基层卫生保健系统,对于减轻气候驱动的虫媒病毒传播健康影响、保护脆弱人群至关重要。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
气候变化已成为二十一世纪最严峻的公共卫生挑战之一,深刻影响着全球传染病的流行病学。本研究关注气候变化背景下虫媒病毒性疾病负担日益加重的问题,并强调对监测与卫生系统的挑战。
设计与对象
本学术论文对气候变化与虫媒病毒性疾病负担日益加重之间的关系进行了批判性分析,强调了流行病学监测系统与基层卫生保健(PHC)所面临的挑战。讨论聚焦于气候变异与媒介增殖之间的生态机制,以及卫生服务的组织层面影响。
核心方法
本论文通过回顾现有文献并讨论其对卫生系统的影响,分析了气候变化对虫媒病毒性疾病(包括基孔肯雅热)的生态与组织层面影响。文中探讨了气候敏感型治理与PHC在应对此类挑战中的作用。
主要结果
这些气候与环境变化已扩大了蚊媒媒介的地理分布范围,延长了传播季节,并增加了热带与亚热带地区暴发的频率与强度。
解释与创新
讨论探索了气候变异与媒介增殖之间的生态机制、卫生服务的组织层面影响、整合式监测的重要性,以及PHC在预防、早期诊断、社区参与与跨部门行动中的战略作用。
科研与公卫意义
在巴西等虫媒病毒已构成显著公共卫生负担的国家,气候变化加剧了既有脆弱性,并为卫生系统带来新的需求,特别是在疾病监测、预防与医疗服务提供方面。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据未报告本研究的具体局限性或证据强度。

Climate Change and Arboviral Diseases: Challenges for Surveillance and Primary Health Care

Authors: Maria Terezinha Gomes Pontes, Edylene Maria Dos Santos Pereira, Bruno Ramos Brum, Maria Aparecida de Almeida Araújo, Thaita Thaisi Zago, Sérgio Raimundo Ernesto Machado, Sheila Érika Ferro Ramalho Nobre, Neide Moreira de Souza
Original abstract (English)
Climate change has emerged as one of the greatest public health challenges of the twenty-first century, profoundly influencing the epidemiology of infectious diseases worldwide. Among the health conditions most directly affected by environmental transformations are arboviral diseases, including dengue, Zika, chikungunya, and yellow fever, whose transmission dynamics are highly sensitive to variations in temperature, rainfall, humidity, urbanization, and ecosystem disruption. These climatic and environmental changes have expanded the geographic distribution of mosquito vectors, prolonged transmission seasons, and increased the frequency and intensity of outbreaks in tropical and subtropical regions. In countries such as Brazil, where arboviruses already represent a significant public health burden, climate change has intensified existing vulnerabilities and imposed new demands on health systems, particularly regarding disease surveillance, prevention, and healthcare delivery. This academic essay critically examines the relationship between climate change and the growing burden of arboviral diseases, emphasizing the challenges faced by epidemiological surveillance systems and Primary Health Care (PHC). The discussion explores the ecological mechanisms linking climate variability to vector proliferation, the organizational implications for health services, the importance of integrated surveillance, and the strategic role of PHC in prevention, early diagnosis, community engagement, and intersectoral action. The essay argues that strengthening climate-sensitive health governance and resilient primary healthcare systems is essential for reducing the health impacts of climate-driven arboviral transmission and protecting vulnerable populations.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Climate change has emerged as one of the greatest public health challenges of the twenty-first century, profoundly influencing the epidemiology of infectious diseases worldwide. The study addresses the growing burden of arboviral diseases, particularly in the context of climate change, and highlights the challenges for surveillance and healthcare systems.
Design and population
This academic essay critically examines the relationship between climate change and the growing burden of arboviral diseases, emphasizing the challenges faced by epidemiological surveillance systems and Primary Health Care (PHC). The discussion focuses on the ecological mechanisms linking climate variability to vector proliferation and the organizational implications for health services.
Core methods
The essay analyzes the ecological and organizational impacts of climate change on arboviral diseases, including chikungunya, by reviewing existing literature and discussing the implications for health systems. It explores the role of climate-sensitive governance and PHC in addressing these challenges.
Main results
These climatic and environmental changes have expanded the geographic distribution of mosquito vectors, prolonged transmission seasons, and increased the frequency and intensity of outbreaks in tropical and subtropical regions.
Interpretation and novelty
The discussion explores the ecological mechanisms linking climate variability to vector proliferation, the organizational implications for health services, the importance of integrated surveillance, and the strategic role of PHC in prevention, early diagnosis, community engagement, and intersectoral action.
Scientific and public-health significance
In countries such as Brazil, where arboviruses already represent a significant public health burden, climate change has intensified existing vulnerabilities and imposed new demands on health systems, particularly regarding disease surveillance, prevention, and healthcare delivery.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations or evidence strength for the study.

加强蚊媒控制以应对新发蚊媒传染病

Strengthening mosquito control to manage emerging mosquito-borne diseases
作者： Olivier Briët, Antero Airaksinen, Laura Gillini, Maria Daniel Loureiro, Jeanne Vuaille, Vincent Delvaux, Ana Margarida Alho, Áine Collins, Céline M Gossner
摘要中文翻译登革热、基孔肯雅热和西尼罗河病毒感染等蚊媒传染病在欧洲日益频发。这些疫情发展要求各国及各部门间具备充分准备与协调响应机制。蚊媒控制是公共卫生响应的核心组成部分,既用于遏制蚊媒传播,又用于降低疾病暴发风险。尽管蚊媒控制至关重要,但大多数方法的成本效益证据仍有限,这使得基于证据的蚊媒管理策略设计变得复杂。此外,获批的蚊媒控制产品范围狭窄,且因杀虫剂抗性问题日益严重。欧洲国家的蚊媒控制涉及多元化参与者,而欧盟层面的蚊媒传染病防控工作则由多个职能互补的机构共同开展。因此,欧洲应通过‘一个健康’方法推动跨部门协作,将公共卫生、环境健康、兽医健康与城市规划部门整合,同时动员社区参与蚊媒监测与控制。为制定可持续的、科学的综合蚊媒控制策略以保护公共卫生、动物健康与生态系统,需持续投入研究以评估现有及新型蚊媒控制方法的成本效益、安全性与生态影响。监管政策应为生物杀灭剂、非生物杀灭剂及创新控制方法提供清晰且灵活的框架,以支持上述努力。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热、基孔肯雅热和西尼罗河病毒感染等蚊媒传染病在欧洲日益频发。这些疫情发展要求各国及各部门间具备充分准备与协调响应机制。
设计与对象
本研究聚焦欧洲国家的蚊媒控制策略,涉及多元化参与者,并涵盖多个职能互补的机构。
核心方法
本研究审视蚊媒控制的现状,包括获批蚊媒控制产品的范围及杀虫剂抗性带来的挑战。
主要结果
大多数蚊媒控制方法的成本效益证据仍有限,这使得基于证据的蚊媒管理策略设计变得复杂。
解释与创新
作者认为,获批蚊媒控制产品范围狭窄且杀虫剂抗性日益严重,亟需开发新的蚊媒控制方法。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调需持续投入研究,以评估现有及新型蚊媒控制方法的成本效益、安全性与生态影响。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据未报告具体数值结果或详细统计分析。

Strengthening mosquito control to manage emerging mosquito-borne diseases

Authors: Olivier Briët, Antero Airaksinen, Laura Gillini, Maria Daniel Loureiro, Jeanne Vuaille, Vincent Delvaux, Ana Margarida Alho, Áine Collins, Céline M Gossner
Original abstract (English)
Mosquito-borne diseases such as dengue, chikungunya and West Nile virus infection become increasingly frequent in Europe. These developments require preparedness and coordinated response mechanisms across countries and sectors. Vector control is a central component of public health response, both for containing outbreaks and for reducing the risk of disease emergence. While mosquito control is important, evidence on the cost-effectiveness of most methods remains limited, complicating the design of evidence-based vector management strategies. In addition, the range of authorised vector control products is narrow and increasingly compromised by insecticide resistance. Mosquito control in European countries involves a diverse set of actors, and work on mosquito-borne diseases at European Union level spans several institutions with complementary mandates. Europe should therefore promote cross-sectoral collaboration through a One Health approach that brings together public health, environmental health, veterinary health and urban planning sectors, while engaging communities in mosquito surveillance and control. Continued investment in research to assess the cost-effectiveness, safety and ecological impact of existing and new vector control methods is needed to develop sustainable, science-based, integrated mosquito control strategies that protect public health, animal health and ecosystems. Regulatory clarity and flexibility, for biocidal, non-biocidal and innovative control approaches, would support this effort.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Mosquito-borne diseases such as dengue, chikungunya and West Nile virus infection become increasingly frequent in Europe. These developments require preparedness and coordinated response mechanisms across countries and sectors.
Design and population
The study focuses on mosquito control strategies in European countries, involving a diverse set of actors and spanning several institutions with complementary mandates.
Core methods
The study examines the current state of mosquito control, including the range of authorised vector control products and the challenges posed by insecticide resistance.
Main results
Evidence on the cost-effectiveness of most mosquito control methods remains limited, complicating the design of evidence-based vector management strategies.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that the narrow range of authorised vector control products and increasing insecticide resistance necessitate new approaches to mosquito control.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the need for continued investment in research to assess the cost-effectiveness, safety and ecological impact of existing and new vector control methods.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific numerical findings or detailed statistical analyses.

共流行环境中的 基孔肯雅病毒 血清学交叉反应:机制、诊断意义和流行病学后果

Chikungunya Virus serological cross-reactivity in co-endemic settings: Mechanisms, Diagnostic implications, and epidemiological consequences
作者： Ikeoluwa Feyisayo Aina, Oluyinka Oladele Opaleye, Dorcas Ayomide Atilade, Foluke Julianah Adetutu
摘要中文翻译基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 会导致急性发热性疾病,并导致 关节痛 衰弱,血清学检测仍然是 诊断 和 监测 的核心。然而,在多种虫媒病毒共同循环的环境中,基于抗体的结果因交叉反应而变得复杂。本综述区分了影响 CHIKV 血清学 的三种相关但不同的现象:生物交叉反应性,由相关病毒之间真正的抗原重叠引起;与检测相关的非目标反应性,其中诊断平台在病毒之间没有建立生物学关系的情况下产生阳性信号;诊断错误分类,将两者混为一谈的下游流行病学后果。将该框架应用于五个病毒对,我们发现CHIKV-O'nyong-nyong病毒(ONNV)、CHIKV-Mayaro病毒(MAYV)和CHIKV-罗斯河病毒(RRV)关系得到了共享的甲病毒抗原结构的支持,并且在一些研究中还得到了功能性交叉中和的支持。相比之下,登革热病毒 (DENV) 和寨卡病毒 (ZIKV) 相关血清中报道的 CHIKV 反应性缺乏既定的生物学基础,最好归类为未解决机制的检测相关非靶标反应性。这些区别很重要:未经调整的血清阳性率可能会夸大或掩盖 CHIKV 传播 的估计值,并且没有一个诊断平台可以解决每个病毒对的问题。我们认为,共流行环境中的 CHIKV 诊断 需要分层策略:急性期进行分子检测,随后进行抗原告知和多重 血清学,以及基于中和的确认(其中归因至关重要),并结合 监测 的交叉反应感知统计模型。加强分析验证和确认性检测,特别是在尼日利亚等 CHIKV、DENV 和相关甲病毒共同传播的非洲地区,对于提高诊断准确性和流行病学推断至关重要。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述探讨了在共同流行区背景下,基孔肯雅病毒(基孔肯雅病毒)诊断 与 监测 的相互作用,重点关注血清学交叉反应(与相关虫媒病毒)如何影响基孔肯雅病毒感染的诊断准确性及流行病学推断。
证据基础与方法
本综述区分了三种影响基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV)血清学的现象:生物学交叉反应性、检测相关非靶向反应性以及诊断误分类,并将此框架应用于五对病毒以评估其对基孔肯雅病毒血清学诊断的影响。
共识与结论
基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV)与奥尼永-尼永病毒(ONNV)、马亚罗病毒(MAYV)及罗斯河病毒(RRV)共享抗原结构,这由甲病毒属共同的抗原结构所支持,并在多项研究中通过功能性交叉中和作用得到验证;而登革病毒(DENV)和寨卡病毒(ZIKV)相关血清中报道的反应性缺乏已确立的生物学基础,更应归类为检测相关的非靶向反应。
争议与缺口
该综述指出,未经调整的血清流行率可能会高估或掩盖基孔肯雅病毒 传播 的估计值,且单一诊断平台无法解决每对病毒的问题,表明在检测方法验证与确认性检测方面仍存在未解决的挑战,特别是在尼日利亚等基孔肯雅病毒、登革病毒及相关甲病毒共同流行的非洲地区。
科研与实践启示
提出了一种分层诊断策略:急性期进行分子检测,此后采用抗原信息指导的多重血清学检测,并在归因判定关键时通过中和试验进行确认,同时结合针对监测的交叉反应感知统计建模,并加强共同流行区的检测验证与确认检测。

Chikungunya Virus serological cross-reactivity in co-endemic settings: Mechanisms, Diagnostic implications, and epidemiological consequences

Authors: Ikeoluwa Feyisayo Aina, Oluyinka Oladele Opaleye, Dorcas Ayomide Atilade, Foluke Julianah Adetutu
Original abstract (English)
Chikungunya virus (CHIKV) causes an acute febrile illness with debilitating arthralgia, and serological assays remain central to its diagnosis and surveillance. However, antibody-based results are complicated by cross-reactivity in settings where multiple arboviruses co-circulate. This review distinguishes three related but distinct phenomena affecting CHIKV serology: biological cross-reactivity, arising from genuine antigenic overlap between related viruses; assay-associated non-target reactivity, in which a diagnostic platform generates a positive signal without an established biological relationship between the viruses; and diagnostic misclassification, the downstream epidemiological consequence of conflating the two. Applying this framework to five virus pairs, we find that CHIKV–O'nyong-nyong virus (ONNV), CHIKV–Mayaro virus (MAYV) and CHIKV–Ross River virus (RRV) relationships are supported by shared alphavirus antigenic architecture and, in several studies, by functional cross-neutralisation. By contrast, CHIKV reactivity reported in dengue virus (DENV)- and Zika virus (ZIKV)-associated sera lacks an established biological basis and is better classified as assay-associated non-target reactivity of unresolved mechanism. These distinctions matter: unadjusted seropositivity can inflate or obscure CHIKV transmission estimates, and no single diagnostic platform resolves the problem for every virus pair. We argue that CHIKV diagnosis in co-endemic settings requires a layered strategy: molecular detection during the acute phase, antigenically informed and multiplex serology thereafter, and neutralisation-based confirmation where attribution is critical, combined with cross-reactivity-aware statistical modelling for surveillance. Strengthening assay validation and confirmatory testing, particularly in African settings such as Nigeria where CHIKV, DENV and related alphaviruses circulate together, is essential for improving diagnostic accuracy and epidemiological inference.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review examines chikungunya virus (CHIKV) diagnosis and surveillance in co-endemic settings, focusing on the central question of how serological cross-reactivity with related arboviruses affects diagnostic accuracy and epidemiological inference for CHIKV infection.
Evidence base and method
This is a narrative review that distinguishes three phenomena affecting CHIKV serology: biological cross-reactivity, assay-associated non-target reactivity, and diagnostic misclassification, and applies this framework to five virus pairs to assess their impact on CHIKV serological diagnostics.
Consensus and conclusions
CHIKV shares antigenic architecture with O'nyong-nyong virus (ONNV), Mayaro virus (MAYV), and Ross River virus (RRV), supported by shared alphavirus antigenic structure and, in several studies, by functional cross-neutralisation, whereas reactivity reported in dengue virus (DENV)- and Zika virus (ZIKV)-associated sera lacks an established biological basis and is better classified as assay-associated non-target reactivity.
Controversies and gaps
The review highlights that unadjusted seropositivity can inflate or obscure CHIKV transmission estimates, and no single diagnostic platform resolves the problem for every virus pair, indicating unresolved challenges in assay validation and confirmatory testing, particularly in African settings such as Nigeria where CHIKV, DENV, and related alphaviruses co-circulate.
Research and practice implications
A layered diagnostic strategy is proposed: molecular detection during the acute phase, antigenically informed and multiplex serology thereafter, and neutralisation-based confirmation where attribution is critical, combined with cross-reactivity-aware statistical modelling for surveillance, alongside strengthening assay validation and confirmatory testing in co-endemic regions.

评审员 #2(公开评审):巨环结构域催化活性调控基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV)传播及其在伊蚊中的潜在致病性

Reviewer #2 (Public review): Macrodomain catalytic activity modulates Chikungunya virus dissemination and transmission potential in Aedes mosquitoes
作者： Eugenia S. Bardossy, Lena Bergmann, Annabelle Henrion-Lacritick, Jared Nigg, Galen J Correy, Alan Ashworth, James S. Fraser, Maria‐Carla Saleh
摘要中文翻译病毒巨环结构域是具有前景的抗病毒药物靶标,其可对抗哺乳动物 ADP-核糖基化介导的抗病毒应答。然而,其在蚊媒双顺反子病毒中的作用仍鲜为人知。本研究通过将基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV)非结构蛋白3(nsP3)巨环结构域活性位点天冬酰胺24(N24)突变,探究其催化活性的作用。在哺乳动物 Vero 细胞中,N24 突变株病毒快速选择了天冬氨酸31(D31)处的第二位点回复突变,而在干扰素反应能力正常的人细胞中则倾向于在24位点发生回复突变,提示巨环结构域功能受损后的适应性响应。催化失活的双突变株病毒在干扰素反应能力正常的人细胞中复制能力显著下降,但在蚊细胞中复制能力与野生型相当。在活体伊蚊中,巨环结构域回复突变以突变体特异性方式调控 CHIKV 复制和感染性:一种突变体降低了复制和感染性,而另一种则增强了。生化和结构分析证实,这些表型源于催化失活但仍保留 ADP-核糖结合能力的巨环结构域,其中 D31 回复突变位于底物出口通路。研究结果表明,巨环结构域催化活性调控 CHIKV 在伊蚊中的传播及其潜在致病性,揭示了催化失活的病毒酶在哺乳动物细胞中存在进化优势差异。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本研究聚焦基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV),具体探讨其非结构蛋白3(nsP3)巨环结构域催化活性在调控病毒传播及其在伊蚊宿主中潜在致病性中的作用。核心问题是巨环结构域的催化活性是否影响 CHIKV 在蚊媒宿主中的复制和感染性。
证据基础与方法
本评审基于单项实验研究。研究设计包括对 CHIKV nsP3 巨环结构域活性位点天冬酰胺24(N24)进行突变,并分析所得病毒表型在哺乳动物 Vero 细胞和活体伊蚊中的变化。证据来源于对 CHIKV 突变株的直接实验操作及体内测试。
共识与结论
研究得出结论:巨环结构域催化活性调控 CHIKV 在伊蚊中的传播及其潜在致病性。该结论得到以下发现的支持:巨环结构域回复突变对蚊细胞中病毒复制和感染性的突变体特异性效应,以及催化失活的双突变株病毒在蚊细胞中复制能力与野生型相当,但在干扰素反应能力正常的人细胞中复制能力显著下降。
争议与缺口
证据显示 CHIKV 对巨环结构域功能受损的适应性响应存在异质性,在哺乳动物和蚊细胞中观察到不同结果。然而,该研究未涉及这些发现在其他 CHIKV 谱系或其他蚊种中的更广泛意义,导致对结果普遍性的理解存在缺口。
科研与实践启示
研究结果提示,靶向巨环结构域催化活性可能成为抗病毒药物干预的潜在策略。亟需进一步研究以探索巨环结构域活性在不同 CHIKV 谱系和蚊种中的作用,以及这些回复突变对病毒潜在致病性动态的影响。

Reviewer #2 (Public review): Macrodomain catalytic activity modulates Chikungunya virus dissemination and transmission potential in Aedes mosquitoes

Authors: Eugenia S. Bardossy, Lena Bergmann, Annabelle Henrion-Lacritick, Jared Nigg, Galen J Correy, Alan Ashworth, James S. Fraser, Maria‐Carla Saleh
Original abstract (English)
Viral macrodomains are promising antiviral targets that counteract host ADP-ribosylation-mediated antiviral responses in mammals. However, their role in dual-host viruses within the mosquito vector remains largely unknown. Here, we investigated the role of the Chikungunya virus (CHIKV) nsP3 macrodomain catalytic activity by mutating the active-site asparagine 24 (N24). N24 mutant viruses rapidly selected for compensatory second-site mutations at aspartate 31 (D31) in mammalian Vero cells, while interferon-competent human cells favored reversion at position 24 instead, suggesting adaptive responses to macrodomain functional impairment. Catalytically inactive double-mutant viruses replicated poorly in interferon-competent human cells but comparably to wild-type in mosquito cells. In Aedes mosquitoes in vivo, macrodomain mutations modulated CHIKV replication and infectivity in a mutation-specific manner: one mutant reduced them while another enhanced them. Biochemical and structural analyses confirmed that these phenotypes arise in the context of a catalytically inactive macrodomain that retains ADP-ribose binding capacity, with D31 mutations lining the substrate exit path. These findings demonstrate that macrodomain catalytic activity modulates CHIKV dissemination and transmission potential in Aedes mosquitoes, revealing fundamental differences in host-dependent evolutionary advantage for a catalytically inactive viral enzyme.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The study focuses on Chikungunya virus (CHIKV), specifically examining the role of the nsP3 macrodomain catalytic activity in modulating virus dissemination and transmission potential in Aedes mosquitoes. The central question is whether the catalytic activity of the macrodomain influences CHIKV replication and infectivity in mosquito vectors.
Evidence base and method
The review is based on a single experimental study. The study design involved mutating the active-site asparagine 24 (N24) of the CHIKV nsP3 macrodomain and analyzing the resulting viral phenotypes in mammalian Vero cells and Aedes mosquitoes. The evidence is derived from direct experimental manipulation and in vivo testing of CHIKV mutants.
Consensus and conclusions
The study concludes that macrodomain catalytic activity modulates CHIKV dissemination and transmission potential in Aedes mosquitoes. This is supported by findings that show mutation-specific effects on viral replication and infectivity in mosquito cells, with catalytically inactive double-mutant viruses replicating comparably to wild-type in mosquito cells but poorly in interferon-competent human cells.
Controversies and gaps
The evidence highlights heterogeneity in the adaptive responses of CHIKV to macrodomain functional impairment, with different outcomes observed in mammalian and mosquito cells. However, the study does not address the broader implications of these findings across other CHIKV lineages or other mosquito species, leaving gaps in understanding the generalizability of the results.
Research and practice implications
The findings suggest that targeting the macrodomain catalytic activity could be a potential strategy for antiviral interventions. Further research is needed to explore the role of macrodomain activity in different CHIKV lineages and mosquito species, as well as to assess the impact of these mutations on viral transmission dynamics in natural settings.

eLife 评估:巨域催化活性调控基孔肯雅病毒在伊蚊中的扩散与传播潜能

eLife Assessment: Macrodomain catalytic activity modulates Chikungunya virus dissemination and transmission potential in Aedes mosquitoes
作者： Melody Man Hing Li
摘要中文翻译病毒巨环结构域是具有前景的抗病毒药物靶标,其可对抗哺乳动物 ADP-核糖基化介导的抗病毒应答。然而,其在蚊媒双顺反子病毒中的作用仍鲜为人知。本研究通过将基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV)非结构蛋白3(nsP3)巨环结构域活性位点天冬酰胺24(N24)突变,探究其催化活性的作用。在哺乳动物 Vero 细胞中,N24 突变株病毒快速选择了天冬氨酸31(D31)处的第二位点回复突变,而在干扰素反应能力正常的人细胞中则倾向于在24位点发生回复突变,提示巨环结构域功能受损后的适应性响应。催化失活的双突变株病毒在干扰素反应能力正常的人细胞中复制能力显著下降,但在蚊细胞中复制能力与野生型相当。在活体伊蚊中,巨环结构域回复突变以突变体特异性方式调控 CHIKV 复制和感染性:一种突变体降低了复制和感染性,而另一种则增强了。生化和结构分析证实,这些表型源于催化失活但仍保留 ADP-核糖结合能力的巨环结构域,其中 D31 回复突变位于底物出口通路。研究结果表明,巨环结构域催化活性调控 CHIKV 在伊蚊中的传播及其潜在致病性,揭示了催化失活的病毒酶在哺乳动物细胞中存在进化优势差异。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本研究聚焦基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV),探讨其非结构蛋白3(nsP3)巨域催化活性如何影响其在伊蚊中的扩散与传播潜能。核心问题是巨域催化活性是否在调控该病毒在上述宿主中的复制与感染性方面发挥作用。
证据基础与方法
本评估基于对基孔肯雅病毒nsP3巨域催化活性的实验研究,重点针对其活性位点天冬酰胺24(N24)。证据包括在哺乳动物Vero细胞中的体外实验及在伊蚊中的体内研究。研究方法涉及生化与结构分析,以阐明这些突变对功能的影响。
共识与结论
证据支持巨域催化活性可调控基孔肯雅病毒在伊蚊中的复制与感染性。巨域内特定位点(如N24与D31)的突变在不同宿主环境中导致不同结果;D31突变沿底物出口通路排列,并以突变特异性方式影响病毒功能。
争议与缺口
证据表明,巨域催化活性在基孔肯雅病毒等双宿主病毒中的作用尚未完全明确。部分突变导致复制减弱,而其他突变则增强复制,提示潜在的适应性响应。本研究未涉及这些发现在其他相关病毒或自然生态系统中的更广泛影响。
科研与实践启示
研究结果提示,靶向巨域催化活性可能成为抗病毒药物研发的潜在策略。需进一步探索巨域突变在其他相关病毒中的功能角色,并评估其对病毒传播动态的影响。

eLife Assessment: Macrodomain catalytic activity modulates Chikungunya virus dissemination and transmission potential in Aedes mosquitoes

Authors: Melody Man Hing Li
Original abstract (English)
Viral macrodomains are promising antiviral targets that counteract host ADP-ribosylation-mediated antiviral responses in mammals. However, their role in dual-host viruses within the mosquito vector remains largely unknown. Here, we investigated the role of the Chikungunya virus (CHIKV) nsP3 macrodomain catalytic activity by mutating the active-site asparagine 24 (N24). N24 mutant viruses rapidly selected for compensatory second-site mutations at aspartate 31 (D31) in mammalian Vero cells, while interferon-competent human cells favored reversion at position 24 instead, suggesting adaptive responses to macrodomain functional impairment. Catalytically inactive double-mutant viruses replicated poorly in interferon-competent human cells but comparably to wild-type in mosquito cells. In Aedes mosquitoes in vivo, macrodomain mutations modulated CHIKV replication and infectivity in a mutation-specific manner: one mutant reduced them while another enhanced them. Biochemical and structural analyses confirmed that these phenotypes arise in the context of a catalytically inactive macrodomain that retains ADP-ribose binding capacity, with D31 mutations lining the substrate exit path. These findings demonstrate that macrodomain catalytic activity modulates CHIKV dissemination and transmission potential in Aedes mosquitoes, revealing fundamental differences in host-dependent evolutionary advantage for a catalytically inactive viral enzyme.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The study focuses on Chikungunya virus (CHIKV) and investigates how the nsP3 macrodomain catalytic activity influences its dissemination and transmission potential in Aedes mosquitoes. The central question is whether the catalytic activity of the macrodomain plays a role in modulating viral replication and infectivity in these vectors.
Evidence base and method
The review is based on experimental studies involving mutation of the nsP3 macrodomain in CHIKV, specifically targeting the active-site asparagine 24 (N24). The evidence includes in vitro experiments in mammalian Vero cells and in vivo studies in Aedes mosquitoes. The methods involve biochemical and structural analyses to understand the functional implications of these mutations.
Consensus and conclusions
The evidence supports that macrodomain catalytic activity modulates CHIKV replication and infectivity in Aedes mosquitoes. Mutations in the macrodomain, particularly N24 and D31, lead to distinct outcomes in different host environments, with D31 mutations lining the substrate exit path and affecting viral function in a mutation-specific manner.
Controversies and gaps
The evidence highlights that the role of macrodomain catalytic activity in dual-host viruses like CHIKV is not fully understood. While some mutations lead to reduced replication, others enhance it, indicating potential adaptive responses. The study does not address the broader implications of these findings in other related viruses or in natural ecosystems.
Research and practice implications
The findings suggest that targeting the macrodomain catalytic activity could be a potential strategy for antiviral development. Further research is needed to explore the functional roles of macrodomain mutations in other related viruses and to assess their impact on viral transmission dynamics in natural settings.

基孔肯雅病毒:输血服务机构需了解的信息

Chikungunya Virus: What a Transfusion Service Needs to Know
作者： Daniele Focosi, Giuseppe Sberna, Francesca Colavita, Fabrizio Maggi, Massimo Franchini
摘要中文翻译披沙病毒科(Togaviridae)是可能引发国际关注的突发公共卫生事件的候选病毒科之一。其中,基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV,CHKIV)已在大多数大陆的温带地区引发反复流行,并对血液供应安全构成威胁。伊蚊(Aedes mosquitoes,媒介)的全球扩散及国际旅行的增加,推动了该病原体流行病学重要性的上升。实际上,基孔肯雅病毒已在110余个国家报告,涉及以下四个大洲:非洲、亚洲、美洲及欧洲。因此,公共卫生当局强调加强媒介控制计划(监测)、伊蚊(媒介)控制计划、准备工作(暴发)及血液安全措施的重要性。本综述旨在总结基孔肯雅病毒的当前知识,涵盖病毒学、分子进化(流行病学)、发病机制(传播)、临床特征、预防策略及血液安全影响,并特别关注与输血服务相关的问题。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述聚焦基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV)及其相关疾病,针对输血服务机构,总结病毒学、分子进化(流行病学)、发病机制(传播)、临床特征、预防策略及血液安全影响。
证据基础与方法
所提供的证据未报告综述类型、检索的数据库、检索日期、纳入标准、筛选流程或纳入研究数量。
共识与结论
基孔肯雅病毒已在非洲、亚洲、美洲及欧洲110余个国家被检测到,凸显其全球扩散态势。公共卫生当局强调需加强媒介控制(监测)、伊蚊(媒介)控制计划、准备工作(暴发)及特定血液安全措施,以缓解发病机制(传播)风险。
争议与缺口
所提供的证据未报告关于基孔肯雅病毒分子进化(流行病学)或血液安全干预措施的任何争议、矛盾发现或特定证据缺口。
科研与实践启示
当局应优先开展基孔肯雅病毒的系统媒介控制(监测),加强伊蚊(媒介)控制工作,确保准备工作(暴发)到位,并为输血服务机构实施严格的血液安全方案。

Chikungunya Virus: What a Transfusion Service Needs to Know

Authors: Daniele Focosi, Giuseppe Sberna, Francesca Colavita, Fabrizio Maggi, Massimo Franchini
Original abstract (English)
Togaviridae represents a candidate family for emerging public health emergencies of international concern. Among them, chikungunya virus (CHKIV) has caused recurrent epidemics in the temperate zone of most continents and represents a threat to the safety of blood supplies. The global expansion of vectors (i.e., Aedes mosquitoes) and increasing international travel have contributed to the growing epidemiological importance of this pathogen. In fact, CHIKV has been reported in more than 110 countries across the following four continents: Africa, Asia, Americas, and Europe. Consequently, public health authorities have emphasized the importance of surveillance, vector control programs, outbreak preparedness, and blood safety measures. The aim of this review was to summarize the current knowledge of CHIKV considering virology, molecular epidemiology, transmission mechanisms, clinical features, preventive strategies, and blood safety implications, with particular attention to issues relevant to transfusion services.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on chikungunya virus (CHIKV) and its associated disease, targeting transfusion services by summarizing virology, molecular epidemiology, transmission mechanisms, clinical features, preventive strategies, and blood‐safety implications.
Evidence base and method
The supplied evidence does not report the review type, databases searched, dates, eligibility criteria, selection process, or number of included studies.
Consensus and conclusions
CHIKV has been detected in more than 110 countries across Africa, Asia, the Americas and Europe, underscoring its global spread. Public‐health authorities stress enhanced surveillance, vector‐control programmes, outbreak preparedness and specific blood‐safety measures to mitigate transmission risk.
Controversies and gaps
The supplied evidence does not report any controversies, conflicting findings, or specific evidence gaps regarding CHIKV epidemiology or blood‐safety interventions.
Research and practice implications
Authorities should prioritize systematic surveillance of CHIKV, strengthen vector‐control efforts, ensure outbreak preparedness, and implement rigorous blood‐safety protocols for transfusion services.

非洲人群中虫媒病毒病严重程度的病毒与宿主决定因素:一项荟萃分析

Viral and host determinants of arboviral disease severity in African populations: A systematic review with meta-analysis.
作者： Peter Mac Asaga, Vijeesh Kadukkatti, Philomena Airiohuodion, Dakul Anthony Danaan, Dave C. Ibeh, Axel Kroeger, Peter Asaga Mac
摘要中文翻译背景:由登革病毒(DENV)、基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV)、黄热病病毒(YFV)、裂谷热病毒(RVFV)和寨卡病毒(ZIKV)引起的虫媒病毒疾病在非洲导致了大量发病和死亡率。临床表现从自限性发热疾病到致命性出血综合征、脑炎和慢性关节痛不等,但决定谁会发展为重症的因素在非洲环境中仍不明确。我们旨在综合目前对五种虫媒病毒在非洲人群中临床重症的病毒和宿主免疫决定因素的已知知识。方法:从数据库创建至2026年3月8日,检索PubMed/MEDLINE、Scopus、Web of Science和WHO全球医学索引,以纳入报道DENV、CHIKV、YFV、RVFV或ZIKV感染在非洲人群或有明确非洲血统人群中临床重症结局或病毒和宿主免疫决定因素的研究。纳入标准遵循人群、暴露、比较、结局(PECO)框架。使用纽卡斯尔-渥太华量表(NOS)评估研究质量,并采用GRADE框架评估荟萃分析结局的证据确定性。方案于2026年3月7日在PROSPERO(CRD420261334251)进行前瞻性注册,早于文献检索开始。本综述依据PRISMA 2020指南进行,已完成清单见补充表S2。结果:纳入1978年至2026年间发表的154项研究,覆盖38个非洲及非洲侨民国家。DENV是研究最多的病毒(n=57),其次为RVFV(n=31)、多种虫媒病毒(n=27)、CHIKV(n=15)、YFV(n=15)和ZIKV(n=9)。使用Freeman-Tukey双弧正弦变换的随机效应荟萃分析得出RVFV病例死亡率汇总值为16.9%(95%置信区间11.6至22.8;I2=88.4%,19项研究),以及DENV重症比例汇总值为8.5%(95%置信区间3.0至16.2;I2=95.7%,10项非洲研究)。由于两项分析的异质性极高,这些汇总估计值应被视为可用数据的近似中心趋势,而非精确的流行病学参数,且与亚洲报道的队列研究数据的任何比较均需极其谨慎。两项汇总结局的证据确定性均按GRADE标准评定为“非常低”。Egger检验显示RVFV的漏斗图存在显著不对称(p<0.001)。致命性RVFV和YFV感染的共同特征为细胞因子失调(IL-6、IL-8、MCP-1、IP-10升高)。来自混合图谱分析、Fc受体多态性及临床重症比较的初步假设生成性证据显示,非洲血统人群可能存在遗传介导的登革保护作用,但缺乏在非洲大陆队列研究中的直接验证。非洲地区的ZIKV临床重症数据几乎缺失。结论:在非洲人群中,虫媒病毒重症的免疫病理特征正在显现,包括致命性RVFV和YFV感染的共同细胞因子失调、与非洲血统相关的遗传介导登革保护作用的初步证据,以及长期CHIKV关节痛。然而,现有证据基础仍严重倾向于描述性流行病学。关键缺口包括ZIKV临床数据的近乎缺失、细胞因子和T细胞表型分析的有限性,以及缺乏前瞻性多点研究,这些均需得到解决以指导非洲大陆的疫苗部署、临床管理和大流行防范。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
由登革病毒(DENV)、基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV)、黄热病病毒(YFV)、裂谷热病毒(RVFV)和寨卡病毒(ZIKV)引起的虫媒病毒疾病在非洲导致了显著的发病率和死亡率。临床表现从自限性发热疾病到致命性出血综合征、脑炎和慢性关节痛,然而在非洲环境中,决定何人发展为重症的因素仍不明确。我们旨在综合目前对五种虫媒病毒在非洲人群中临床严重程度的病毒和宿主免疫决定因素的已知知识。
证据基础与方法
从数据库建立至2026年3月8日,检索了PubMed/MEDLINE、Scopus、Web of Science及WHO全球医学索引,以寻找报道登革病毒(DENV)、基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV)、黄热病病毒(YFV)、里夫特山谷热病毒(RVFV)或寨卡病毒(ZIKV)感染在非洲人群或有明确非洲血统人群中临床严重程度结局或病毒或宿主免疫决定因素的研究。纳入资格遵循人群、暴露、比较、结局(PECO)框架。研究质量使用纽卡斯尔-渥太华量表(NOS)评估,而荟萃分析结局的证据确定性则采用GRADE框架评价。本方案于2026年3月7日在PROSPERO(登记号CRD420261334251)进行前瞻性注册,早于文献检索开始。
共识与结论
在非洲人群中,不同的虫媒病毒重症正在显现出独特的免疫病理特征,包括致死性裂谷热病毒(RVFV)和黄热病毒(YFV)的趋同性细胞因子失调、与非洲血统相关的基因介导的登革热保护的初步证据,以及基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV)的长期关节痛。然而,现有证据基础仍严重倾向于描述性的流行病学。
争议与缺口
由于两项分析均存在极端异质性,这些汇总估计值应被视为可用数据的近似中心趋势,而非精确的流行病学参数,且任何与亚洲报告的队列研究数据的比较均需极其谨慎。根据GRADE标准,两项汇总结局的证据确定性均被评定为“非常低”。Egger检验显示RVFV的漏斗图存在显著不对称(p < 0.001)。关键缺口,特别是几乎缺失的临床ZIKV数据、细胞因子与T细胞谱分析的不足,以及缺乏前瞻性多点研究,亟待解决以指导疫苗的部署、临床管理及该大陆的大流行防备工作。
科研与实践启示
在非洲人群中,通过混合图谱分析、Fc受体多态性及临床严重程度比较,发现了初步的、假设生成性证据,提示遗传介导的登革热保护机制,但缺乏在非洲大陆队列研究的直接验证。非洲地区Zika病毒(ZIKV)临床严重程度数据几乎缺失。

Viral and host determinants of arboviral disease severity in African populations: A systematic review with meta-analysis.

Authors: Peter Mac Asaga, Vijeesh Kadukkatti, Philomena Airiohuodion, Dakul Anthony Danaan, Dave C. Ibeh, Axel Kroeger, Peter Asaga Mac
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Arboviral diseases caused by dengue (DENV), chikungunya (CHIKV), yellow fever (YFV), Rift Valley fever (RVFV), and Zika (ZIKV) viruses are responsible for considerable morbidity and mortality across Africa. Clinical presentations range from self-limiting febrile illness to fatal haemorrhagic syndromes, encephalitis, and chronic arthropathy, yet the factors that govern who develops severe disease remain poorly defined in African settings. We set out to synthesise what is currently known about the viral and host immune determinants of clinical severity for all five arboviruses in African populations. METHODS: PubMed/MEDLINE, Scopus, Web of Science, and the WHO Global Index Medicus were searched from database inception to 8 March 2026 for studies reporting clinical severity outcomes or viral or host immune determinants of DENV, CHIKV, YFV, RVFV, or ZIKV infections in African populations or populations of documented African ancestry. Eligibility followed a Population, Exposure, Comparison, Outcome (PECO) framework. Study quality was assessed using the Newcastle-Ottawa Scale (NOS), and the certainty of evidence for meta-analytic outcomes was evaluated using the GRADE framework. The protocol was registered prospectively on PROSPERO (CRD420261334251) on 7 March 2026, before the literature search commenced. The review was conducted in accordance with PRISMA 2020, and the completed checklist is provided as S2 Table. RESULTS: We included 154 studies published between 1978 and 2026, covering 38 African and African-diaspora countries. DENV was the most frequently studied virus (n = 57), followed by RVFV (n = 31), multiple arboviruses (n = 27), CHIKV (n = 15), YFV (n = 15), and ZIKV (n = 9). Random-effects meta-analysis using Freeman-Tukey double arcsine transformation yielded a pooled RVFV case fatality rate of 16.9% (95% CI 11.6 to 22.8; I2 = 88.4%, 19 studies) and a pooled DENV severe disease proportion of 8.5% (95% CI 3.0 to 16.2; I2 = 95.7%, 10 African studies). Given the extreme heterogeneity in both analyses, these pooled estimates should be interpreted as approximate central tendencies of the available data rather than precise epidemiological parameters, and any comparison with reported Asian cohort figures must be made with considerable caution. The certainty of evidence for both pooled outcomes was rated as very low by GRADE criteria. Egger's test indicated significant funnel plot asymmetry for RVFV (p < 0.001). Convergent cytokine dysregulation (elevated IL-6, IL-8, MCP-1, IP-10) characterised fatal RVFV and YFV. Preliminary, hypothesis-generating evidence for genetically mediated dengue protection in African-ancestry populations emerged from admixture mapping, Fc receptor polymorphism, and clinical severity comparisons, though direct validation in continental African cohorts is lacking. ZIKV clinical severity data from Africa were near-absent. CONCLUSIONS: Distinct immunopathological signatures are emerging for arboviral severity in African populations, including convergent cytokine dysregulation in fatal RVFV and YFV, preliminary evidence for genetically mediated dengue protection linked to African ancestry, and prolonged CHIKV arthropathy. The evidence base, however, remains heavily weighted towards descriptive epidemiology. Critical gaps, particularly the near-absence of clinical ZIKV data, limited cytokine and T-cell profiling, and the lack of prospective multi-site studies, must be addressed to inform vaccine deployment, clinical management, and pandemic preparedness on the continent.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Arboviral diseases caused by dengue (DENV), chikungunya (CHIKV), yellow fever (YFV), Rift Valley fever (RVFV), and Zika (ZIKV) viruses are responsible for considerable morbidity and mortality across Africa. Clinical presentations range from self-limiting febrile illness to fatal haemorrhagic syndromes, encephalitis, and chronic arthropathy, yet the factors that govern who develops severe disease remain poorly defined in African settings. We set out to synthesise what is currently known about the viral and host immune determinants of clinical severity for all five arboviruses in African populations.
Evidence base and method
PubMed/MEDLINE, Scopus, Web of Science, and the WHO Global Index Medicus were searched from database inception to 8 March 2026 for studies reporting clinical severity outcomes or viral or host immune determinants of DENV, CHIKV, YFV, RVFV, or ZIKV infections in African populations or populations of documented African ancestry. Eligibility followed a Population, Exposure, Comparison, Outcome (PECO) framework. Study quality was assessed using the Newcastle-Ottawa Scale (NOS), and the certainty of evidence for meta-analytic outcomes was evaluated using the GRADE framework. The protocol was registered prospectively on PROSPERO (CRD420261334251) on 7 March 2026, before the literature search commenced.
Consensus and conclusions
Distinct immunopathological signatures are emerging for arboviral severity in African populations, including convergent cytokine dysregulation in fatal RVFV and YFV, preliminary evidence for genetically mediated dengue protection linked to African ancestry, and prolonged CHIKV arthropathy. The evidence base, however, remains heavily weighted towards descriptive epidemiology.
Controversies and gaps
Given the extreme heterogeneity in both analyses, these pooled estimates should be interpreted as approximate central tendencies of the available data rather than precise epidemiological parameters, and any comparison with reported Asian cohort figures must be made with considerable caution. The certainty of evidence for both pooled outcomes was rated as very low by GRADE criteria. Egger's test indicated significant funnel plot asymmetry for RVFV (p < 0.001). Critical gaps, particularly the near-absence of clinical ZIKV data, limited cytokine and T-cell profiling, and the lack of prospective multi-site studies, must be addressed to inform vaccine deployment, clinical management, and pandemic preparedness on the continent.
Research and practice implications
Preliminary, hypothesis-generating evidence for genetically mediated dengue protection in African-ancestry populations emerged from admixture mapping, Fc receptor polymorphism, and clinical severity comparisons, though direct validation in continental African cohorts is lacking. ZIKV clinical severity data from Africa were near-absent.

基孔肯雅热相关急性呼吸窘迫综合征:一例罕见致死病例 - Cureus

Chikungunya-Associated Acute Respiratory Distress Syndrome: A Rare Fatal Case - Cureus
新闻简报本报告描述了一例罕见致死性基孔肯雅病毒感染并发急性呼吸窘迫综合征(ARDS)的病例,患者为一名62岁男性,合并多种基础疾病。该患者近期曾赴印度勒克瑙旅行,入院时表现为高热、严重关节痛及进行性呼吸困难。入院后患者出现严重低氧血症,需机械通气支持。基孔肯雅病毒IgM抗体检测及RT-PCR结果为阳性,而登革热、疟疾及恙虫病检测均为阴性。患者进展为ARDS、急性肾损伤及多器官功能障碍,住院41天后最终死亡。尽管采用皮质类固醇及肺保护性通气等抗病毒药物治疗,患者病情仍持续恶化,且未检出细菌或真菌感染。该病例凸显基孔肯雅热感染可能引发严重肺部并发症,尤其是在合并多种基础疾病的老年患者中。

时间
该病例报告于2026年发布,患者症状始于近期赴印度勒克瑙旅行后,住院41天后最终死亡。
地点与对象
该病例发生于印度勒克瑙,患者为62岁男性,合并多种基础疾病,包括肥胖、糖尿病、高血压、阻塞性睡眠呼吸暂停、冠心病及间质性肺病。
事件
一例罕见致死性基孔肯雅热相关急性呼吸窘迫综合征(ARDS)病例报告,患者为62岁男性,合并显著基础疾病,旅行后在印度勒克瑙确诊。该病例通过基孔肯雅病毒IgM抗体及RT-PCR检测确认。
规模、影响与风险
该病例涉及严重肺部并发症,包括ARDS,需机械通气支持,最终于住院41天后死亡。患者还出现急性肾损伤、肌阵挛及多器官功能障碍。
应对、状态与不确定性
该病例处于调查阶段,临床医师采用肺保护性通气、俯卧位通气及皮质类固醇等治疗。尽管采用抗病毒药物治疗,患者病情仍持续恶化,且未检出细菌或真菌感染。该病例凸显基孔肯雅热感染可能引发严重肺部并发症,尤其是在合并基础疾病的患者中。

Chikungunya-Associated Acute Respiratory Distress Syndrome: A Rare Fatal Case - Cureus

News Brief
This report describes a rare and fatal case of chikungunya virus infection complicated by acute respiratory distress syndrome (ARDS) in a 62-year-old man with multiple comorbidities. The patient, who had recently traveled to Lucknow, India, presented with high-grade fever, severe arthralgia, and progressive dyspnoea. Upon admission, he was severely hypoxemic and required mechanical ventilation. Chikungunya IgM serology and RT-PCR confirmed the infection, while tests for dengue, malaria, and scrub typhus were negative. The patient developed ARDS, acute kidney injury, and multiorgan dysfunction, ultimately succumbing to the illness after 41 days of hospitalization. Despite treatment with corticosteroids and lung-protective ventilation, his condition worsened, and no bacterial or fungal infection was identified. The case underscores the potential for severe pulmonary complications in chikungunya infection, especially in elderly patients with significant comorbidities.
Original source text (English)
Abstract Chikungunya fever is an arboviral disease caused by an alphavirus belonging to the family Togaviridae. It commonly presents with fever, severe arthralgia, rash, and myalgia, while severe pulmonary involvement causing acute respiratory distress syndrome (ARDS) is rare. We report a 62-year-old man with obesity, diabetes mellitus, hypertension, obstructive sleep apnoea, coronary artery disease, and interstitial lung disease, who presented with high-grade fever, severe arthralgia, facial puffiness, and rapidly progressive dyspnoea, following recent travel to Lucknow, India. On admission, he had severe hypoxemia with a PaO2/FiO2 ratio below 100, requiring mechanical ventilation. During intubation, he developed cardiac arrest but achieved return of spontaneous circulation after cardiopulmonary resuscitation. Chikungunya IgM and reverse transcriptase polymerase chain reaction (RT-PCR) were positive. Chest imaging showed diffuse bilateral infiltrates and ground-glass opacities consistent with severe ARDS. Dengue, malaria, and scrub typhus were excluded. The patient was managed with lung-protective ventilation, prone positioning, and corticosteroids according to the Meduri protocol with transient improvement. However, he later developed worsening respiratory failure and multiorgan dysfunction and eventually succumbed after 41 days of hospitalization. Although chikungunya is not typically considered a respiratory virus, clinicians should recognize that severe pulmonary manifestations, including ARDS, may occur, particularly in elderly patients with significant comorbidities. Introduction Chikungunya fever is an arboviral disease caused by an alphavirus belonging to the family Togaviridae. Historical descriptions of epidemics characterized by fever and severe arthralgia date back to the 18th century, while the first officially documented outbreak occurred in 1952 on the Makonde Plateau in Africa [1]. In India, outbreaks were reported between 1963 and 1973, followed by a prolonged period of relative quiescence until its re-emergence in 2005, initially in southern India and subsequently across multiple regions of the country [2]. Typical clinical manifestations include fever, polyarthralgia, rash, headache, and lymphadenopathy. While most cases are self-limiting, atypical and severe manifestations involving the neurological, cardiovascular, renal, hepatic, and respiratory systems have increasingly been recognized [3]. Acute respiratory distress syndrome (ARDS) is a life-threatening form of acute hypoxemic respiratory failure and a common complication of severe viral pneumonia requiring intensive care. Several respiratory viruses, including influenza viruses, respiratory syncytial virus, rhinoviruses, and coronaviruses, are well-recognized causes of viral pneumonia-associated ARDS [4]. Pulmonary involvement in chikungunya infection is uncommon, while severe pulmonary complications such as respiratory failure and ARDS are rare and have been reported predominantly in severe or atypical cases [3,5]. ARDS secondary to chikungunya infection carries significant morbidity and mortality and remains poorly described in the literature. We present a case of severe chikungunya infection complicated by ARDS in a patient with multiple comorbidities, highlighting the clinical course, management challenges, and outcome. Case Presentation A 62-year-old man presented with high-grade fever for four days associated with severe arthralgia, facial puffiness, redness, and acute-onset progressive breathlessness for five to six hours. He had travelled to Lucknow, India, one week prior to symptom onset. His medical history included morbid obesity, hypertension, type 2 diabetes mellitus, obstructive sleep apnoea, coronary artery disease, and interstitial lung disease. On admission, the patient was severely hypoxemic with a PaO2/FiO2 ratio below 100 and required urgent intubation and invasive mechanical ventilation. During intubation, he developed cardiac arrest and underwent cardiopulmonary resuscitation for eight minutes before achieving return of spontaneous circulation. Given the presentation of fever and severe arthralgia, tropical infectious diseases were investigated. Chikungunya IgM serology and serum reverse transcriptase polymerase chain reaction (RT-PCR) were positive. Dengue, malaria, and scrub typhus testing were negative. A respiratory viral panel, including influenza A and B, SARS-CoV-2, respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) A/B, parainfluenza viruses 1-4, adenovirus, and rhinovirus, was performed and was negative for all tested pathogens. Chest radiography revealed absolute loss of aeration in almost all lung fields due to infiltrates (Figure 1). High-resolution computed tomography (HRCT) demonstrated bilateral consolidations and diffuse ground-glass opacities (Figure 2). Based on the Berlin criteria, he was diagnosed with severe ARDS [6]. The patient was managed with lung-protective ventilation strategies and prone positioning [7]. The patient developed acute kidney injury with oliguria but responded to furosemide challenge, and his oxygenation improved upon proning. The following day, the patient developed myoclonus and was treated with intravenous levetiracetam and midazolam. CT brain was done, which was normal (Figure 3). Intravenous corticosteroid infusion was initiated, considering the severe inflammatory lung injury and refractory hypoxemia, according to the Meduri protocol for ARDS [8]. Significant improvement in oxygenation and chest radiographic findings was observed following steroid therapy (Figure 4). Magnetic resonance imaging of the brain later demonstrated hypoxic-ischemic encephalopathy, likely secondary to the cardiac arrest episode (Figure 5). On Day 11, the patient developed worsening oxygen requirements, accompanied by rising total leukocyte count, procalcitonin, and C-reactive protein levels. Blood and bronchoalveolar lavage culture remained sterile throughout the hospital course, with no microbiological evidence of a concomitant bacterial or fungal infection. Repeat chest radiography demonstrated new pulmonary consolidations (Figure 6). Steroid infusion was discontinued, and hydrocortisone bolus therapy was initiated. Following this, the patient developed progressive multiorgan dysfunction and worsening pulmonary compliance. Repeat HRCT imaging demonstrated extensive progression of lung injury (Figure 7). The patient finally succumbed to his illness after 41 days of hospitalization. Discussion Chikungunya infection is usually self-limiting; however, severe disease can occur in elderly patients, neonates, and those with significant comorbidities [9]. Our patient had multiple risk factors associated with poor outcomes, including obesity, diabetes mellitus, underlying interstitial lung disease, and cardiovascular disease. The chikungunya virus exhibits broad cellular tropism involving endothelial cells, epithelial cells, and fibroblasts, leading to initial activation of innate immune responses and also dissemination of the virus through various lymphoid tissues to other organs [9]. In the acute phase, there is release of various type 1 interferons, as well as upregulation and downregulation of other cytokines [3,9]. Although musculoskeletal involvement remains the hallmark of chikungunya infection, atypical manifestations, such as myocarditis, hepatitis, nephritis, meningoencephalitis, and Guillain-Barré syndrome, have been documented [9]. The viral disease can progress to a chronic phase with prolonged infection of the synovial tissues and muscle cells, as it may survive in such areas of the body that may evade host immune surveillance [9]. Excessive inflammatory responses are believed to contribute to diffuse alveolar damage and ARDS. Pulmonary complications, particularly ARDS, are rare, with only a few reported cases in the literature [5], but are associated with high mortality rates approaching 46% in some reports [3]. Previous reports by Singh et al. and Khare et al. described favourable outcomes in chikungunya-associated ARDS with supportive care and corticosteroid therapy [5,10]. Similarly, our patient initially demonstrated improvement after corticosteroid administration according to the Meduri protocol, suggesting a possible beneficial role of immunomodulation in selected patients with severe inflammatory lung disease. However, the presence of severe comorbidities and hypoxic brain injury likely contributed to the poor outcome. Conclusions Chikungunya fever can rarely present with severe pulmonary involvement leading to ARDS and multiorgan dysfunction. This case emphasizes that respiratory manifestations should not exclude chikungunya from the differential diagnosis in endemic settings, particularly when accompanied by fever and severe arthralgia during outbreaks or following travel exposure. Early recognition of atypical respiratory manifestations, prompt intensive care support, and timely initiation of appropriate therapies may improve outcomes. References - Jagadesh A, Jayaram A, Babu N, et al.: Current status of chikungunya in India. 西班牙报告基孔肯雅热:20例感染病例源自古巴 - CiberCuba

Chikungunya in Spain: 20 of the detected cases originated from Cuba - CiberCuba
新闻简报截至2026年8月16日,西班牙已报告119例基孔肯雅热病例,较2025年同期的42例增长近3倍。其中,20例感染病例源自古巴,另有54例与玻利维亚相关。所有感染均为境外输入病例,西班牙境内未出现本地感染。卫生当局已加强对白纹伊蚊(Aedes albopictus)的监测,该蚊种为该疾病的主要媒介,在西班牙大部分地区(尤其是地中海沿岸地区)广泛分布。主要担忧在于该病毒可能不再仅限于境外输入,从而导致本地传播。始于2025年7月并在古巴16个省中的15个省传播的古巴基孔肯雅热疫情已被确认为西半球病毒输出的重要来源。本地感染的风险在蚊媒活跃季(4月至11月)期间升高,该时段与国际旅行高峰重叠。当局建议清除积水、使用驱虫剂并在黎明和黄昏时段采取防护措施以预防感染。出现症状的旅行者应就医并告知医生其旅行史。
查看新闻五要素
时间
截至2026年8月16日,西班牙报告119例基孔肯雅热病例,较2025年同期的42例增长近3倍。西班牙境内白纹伊蚊活跃期为4月至11月,夏季月份风险最高。古巴基孔肯雅热疫情始于2025年7月,至2026年1月已扩散至16个省中的15个省。
地点与对象
西班牙,具体为加泰罗尼亚自治区、巴伦西亚自治区和马德里自治区,上述地区报告病例数最多。受影响人群包括从古巴及其他地区返回的旅行者,其中54例感染病例源自玻利维亚,20例源自古巴。
事件
西班牙基孔肯雅热病例激增,其中20例感染病例源自古巴。所有感染均为境外输入病例,西班牙境内未出现本地感染病例。病例数上升促使卫生当局加强对白纹伊蚊的监测,该蚊种为该疾病的主要传播媒介。
规模、影响与风险
截至2026年8月16日,西班牙报告119例基孔肯雅热病例,其中20例可追溯至古巴。主要担忧在于该病毒可能不再仅限于境外输入,从而导致本地感染。白纹伊蚊在西班牙大部分地区(尤其是地中海沿岸地区)广泛分布,活跃期为4月至11月。
应对、状态与不确定性
西班牙卫生当局正加强对白纹伊蚊的监测,并建议民众采取清除积水、使用驱虫剂及穿着防护服等预防措施。本地感染风险已被认可但尚未确认。古巴基孔肯雅热疫情已被记录为西半球病毒输出的主要来源。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Chikungunya in Spain: 20 of the detected cases originated from Cuba - CiberCuba

News Brief
Spain has reported 119 cases of chikungunya as of August 16, 2026, nearly tripling the 42 cases recorded during the same period in 2025. Of these, 20 cases originated from Cuba, while 54 were linked to Bolivia. All infections are imported, with no local transmission within Spain. Health authorities have intensified monitoring of the tiger mosquito (Aedes albopictus), the primary vector of the disease, which is prevalent in much of the country, especially in the Mediterranean region. The main concern is that the virus may stop being exclusively imported, which could lead to local spread. The Cuban outbreak, which began in July 2025 and spread to 15 of its 16 provinces, has been identified as a significant source of virus exportation in the Western Hemisphere. The risk of local transmission is heightened during the mosquito's active season, from April to November, which coincides with peak international travel. Authorities recommend eliminating standing water, using repellent, and taking precautions at dawn and dusk to prevent infection. Travelers experiencing symptoms should consult healthcare centers and inform them of their travel history.
Original source text (English)
Homepage Latest News Cuba News Miami News Blackouts today Informal Exchange Rate Videos Sports International News Opinion CiberCuba Interviews United States Cuisine Florida Spain Culture Engine Readings Tourism History Nature Health Close the sidebar ESENFRIT Chikungunya in Spain: 20 of the detected cases originated from Cuba Latest News By CiberCuba Editorial Team Saturday, August 22, 2026 - 8:15 PM Chikungunya fever is transmitted through mosquito bitesPhoto © CiberCuba Spain registered 119 cases of chikungunya as of August 16 of this year, a figure that nearly triples the 42 positives recorded during the same period in 2025, according to data reported to the National Epidemiological Surveillance Network and collected by La Razón. Of the cases with identified origins, 54 come from Bolivia and 20 from Cuba. All infections are imported—none have originated within Spanish territory—but the rise in cases has prompted health authorities to strengthen monitoring of the tiger mosquito (Aedes albopictus), the primary transmitter of the disease, which is present in much of the country, especially in the Mediterranean region. The autonomous communities with the highest number of cases are Catalonia, with 30 positives, followed by the Valencian Community, with 27, and Madrid, with 13. The main concern of epidemiologists is that the virus stops being exclusively imported. For that to happen, it would be enough for a local tiger mosquito to bite an infected person and then another healthy one. Spain has already recorded episodes of indigenous dengue transmission, and France reported this year to the European Centre for Disease Prevention and Control one case of dengue and 15 cases of indigenous chikungunya. Federico Arribas, from the Training group of the Spanish Society of Epidemiology, acknowledges the risk but clarifies: "When a case like this appears, they are very well monitored by our health system." The Cuban context is crucial for understanding the origin of the imported cases. The island suffered a massive chikungunya outbreak that began in July 2025 in Matanzas and spread to 15 of the 16 provinces in the country. The Pan American Health Organization reported in February 2026 a total of 51,217 suspected cases, 1,959 confirmed by laboratory tests, and 46 deaths up to epidemiological week 53 of 2025. In January 2026, an additional 1,457 cases were reported along with two new deaths. The magnitude of the outbreak turned Cuba into one of the main centers for virus exportation in the Western Hemisphere. The GeoSentinel network documented 111 travelers who contracted chikungunya on the island between September 2025 and January 2026, and 64.2% were potentially viremic upon returning to their home countries, confirming the actual risk of international spread. In January 2026, Argentina detected an imported case in a 47-year-old woman from the province of Córdoba who had recently traveled to Cuba, indicating that the island was already exporting the virus to other countries in the region. The tiger mosquito remains active in Spain from April to November, coinciding with the peak of international travel in summer, making these months the period of highest risk for local transmission. In the absence of a vaccine against chikungunya, authorities recommend eliminating standing water around the home, using repellent, wearing clothing that covers arms and legs, and taking extra precautions at dawn and dusk. Those who experience symptoms—high fever, severe joint pain, or headaches—after traveling should visit a healthcare center and inform them of their travel destination. "As citizens, it is also our responsibility to take care of our health," emphasizes Arribas, who recommends checking the epidemiological situation of the destination country before traveling and visiting an International Vaccination Center for advice. Time
As of August 16, 2026, Spain reported 119 cases of chikungunya, nearly tripling the 42 cases recorded during the same period in 2025. The tiger mosquito remains active in Spain from April to November, with the highest risk during summer months. The outbreak in Cuba began in July 2025 and spread to 15 of 16 provinces by January 2026.
Location and population
Spain, specifically the autonomous communities of Catalonia, the Valencian Community, and Madrid, which had the highest number of cases. The affected population includes travelers returning from Cuba and other regions, with 54 cases from Bolivia and 20 from Cuba identified as the origin of infections.
Event
Spain has experienced an increase in chikungunya cases, with 20 of the detected cases originating from Cuba. All infections are imported, and there are no locally originated cases within Spanish territory. The rise in cases has prompted health authorities to strengthen monitoring of the tiger mosquito, which is the primary transmitter of the disease.
Scale, impact and risk
Spain reported 119 cases of chikungunya as of August 16, 2026, with 20 cases traced back to Cuba. The main concern is the potential for the virus to stop being exclusively imported, which could lead to local transmission. The tiger mosquito is present in much of Spain, especially in the Mediterranean region, and remains active from April to November.
Response, status and uncertainty
Health authorities in Spain are strengthening monitoring of the tiger mosquito and advising citizens to take precautions such as eliminating standing water, using repellent, and wearing protective clothing. The risk of local transmission is acknowledged but not confirmed. The Cuban outbreak has been documented as a major source of virus exportation in the Western Hemisphere.

Southwest France steps up mosquito control after 20 chikungunya cases - RFI
新闻简报自2026年8月初以来,法国西南部确认当地感染的基孔肯雅热病例超过20例,当局已加强蚊虫控制措施。该病毒由白纹伊蚊(Aedes albopictus)传播,已在三个省份报告,其中最大的疫情暴发点位于波尔多以北的Prignac-et-Marcamps。卫生当局已启动流行病学调查和现场活动,包括逐户排查,以识别更多病例并提高公众对预防蚊虫叮咬的意识。吉伦特省首例本地感染基孔肯雅热病例发现于2025年夏天,该病毒的传播与更温暖的夏季、城市化进程及国际旅行增加有关。当局建议居民清除自家院落积水,如出现高热、关节或肌肉疼痛、疲劳、头痛或皮疹等症状,应立即就医。此举旨在遏制基孔肯雅热及登革热、寨卡病毒等其他蚊媒疾病的传播。

B routine priorityPublic-health news

Southwest France steps up mosquito control after 20 chikungunya cases - RFI

News Brief
Southwest France has intensified mosquito control measures following the confirmation of over 20 locally contracted chikungunya cases since the start of August 2026. The virus, which is transmitted by the tiger mosquito (Aedes albopictus), has been reported in three departments, with the largest outbreak in Prignac-et-Marcamps, north of Bordeaux. Health authorities have launched epidemiological investigations and field activities, including door-to-door visits, to identify additional cases and raise awareness about preventing mosquito bites. The first locally acquired chikungunya case in Gironde was detected in summer 2025, and the spread of the virus has been attributed to warmer summers, urbanization, and increased international travel. Residents are advised to remove stagnant water from their properties and seek medical attention if they develop symptoms such as high fever, joint or muscle pain, fatigue, headaches, or a skin rash. These efforts aim to curb the spread of chikungunya and other mosquito-borne diseases like dengue and Zika.
Original source text (English)

Issued on: 19/08/2026 - 07:16Modified: 19/08/2026 - 07:17 1 min Reading time A city employee sprays a solution against mosquitos in Saint Denis, Reunion Island, where nearly 100,000 people are thought to have been infected with chikungunya last year. © Adrien/AP By: RFI Advertising Read more The chikungunya cases are described as “autochthonous”, meaning the virus was contracted in France rather than brought back by someone from abroad. The largest-known outbreak of 16 cases is in Prignac-et-Marcamps, north of Bordeaux, where authorities have carried out insecticide treatments in parks and gardens. They are also removing stagnant water where the invasive tiger mosquito lays its eggs. France ramps up fight against tiger mosquitoes after record outbreaks Ideal conditions The tiger mosquito, known scientifically as Aedes albopictus, is originally from Southeast Asia and can transmit chikungunya as well as dengue and Zika. First detected in mainland France in 2004, it is now present across most of the country. The first locally acquired chikungunya case in Gironde was detected in summer 2025, in a five-year-old child. The mosquito’s advance has been fuelled by warmer summers, urbanisation and increased international travel, which together create ideal conditions for both the insect and the viruses it can transmit. Health authorities have been urging residents to remove stagnant water from gardens, balconies and around their homes, as even small amounts of water can become breeding sites. Similar prevention measures have been introduced in the east of the Tarn department after two chikungunya cases were detected, following a case reported at the start of August. What if we killed all mosquitoes? Door-to-door visits In Haute-Garonne, the towns of Cugnaux, Seysses and Brax, near Toulouse, were treated overnight on 10 August following several reports of cases. The Regional Health Agency of Nouvelle-Aquitaine has stressed the importance of investigating confirmed infections. “As soon as a confirmed case of dengue or chikungunya is reported, an epidemiological investigation is launched,” it said in a statement. It added that health teams are carrying out field investigations, including door-to-door visits, in affected neighbourhoods in order to identify other cases and raise awareness on preventing mosquito bites to limit the spread of the diseases. Residents have been advised to seek medical attention promptly if they develop symptoms, which include a high fever, joint or muscle pain, fatigue, headaches or a skin rash. 西班牙基孔肯雅热激增:20例病例与古巴相关

Chikungunya Surge in Spain: 20 Cases Linked to Cuba
新闻简报西班牙报告基孔肯雅热病例激增,截至2026年8月16日累计确诊119例,较2025年同期的42例增长近3倍。其中,20例与古巴相关,54例来自玻利维亚。所有感染均为境外输入,但当局担心通过白纹伊蚊(活跃期为4月至11月)引发本地传播的风险。卫生官员已加强对该蚊种的监测,重点关注病例数量最高的加泰罗尼亚、巴伦西亚自治区和马德里等地区。此次激增凸显了国际传播风险,因古巴在2025年经历了显著的基孔肯雅热暴发,并向其他国家输出了病毒。当局建议采取预防措施,包括清除积水、使用驱虫剂和穿着防护服。目前尚未出现确诊本地传播病例,但风险依然存在,因西班牙此前曾出现本地登革热病例,而法国近期也报告了本地基孔肯雅热病例。医疗系统已做好应对可能出现病例的准备。
查看新闻五要素
时间
截至2026年8月16日,西班牙报告119例基孔肯雅热病例,较2025年同期的42例增长近3倍。白纹伊蚊活跃期为4月至11月,与夏季旅游旺季高峰期重叠,构成本地传播的最高风险期。
地点与对象
西班牙受影响最严重的地区包括加泰罗尼亚、巴伦西亚自治区和马德里。所有感染均为境外输入,其中20例与古巴相关,54例来自玻利维亚。
事件
西班牙已确认119例基孔肯雅热病例,其中20例与古巴相关。此次激增由境外输入病例引发,当局担心通过白纹伊蚊引发本地传播的风险。
规模、影响与风险
此次激增促使卫生官员加强对白纹伊蚊的监测。GeoSentinel网络记录了2025年9月至2026年1月期间111名旅行者在古巴感染基孔肯雅热,其中64.2%在返回时可能处于病毒血症期。存在本地传播风险,如西班牙此前出现的登革热本地病例及法国近期报告的基孔肯雅热本地病例所示。
应对、状态与不确定性
当局建议采取预防措施,包括清除积水、使用驱虫剂和穿着防护服。他们强调医疗系统有效处理病例的能力。本地传播风险仍是关注重点,但目前尚未报告确诊本地病例。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Chikungunya Surge in Spain: 20 Cases Linked to Cuba

News Brief
Spain has reported a surge in chikungunya cases, with 119 cases identified as of August 16, 2026, nearly tripling the 42 cases from the same period in 2025. Among these, 20 cases are linked to Cuba, while 54 are from Bolivia. All infections are imported, and there is concern about the potential for local transmission through the Aedes albopictus mosquito, which is active from April to November. Health officials have intensified monitoring of the mosquito, especially in regions like Catalonia, the Valencian Community, and Madrid, which have the highest case counts. The outbreak highlights the risk of international spread, as Cuba experienced a significant chikungunya outbreak in 2025, exporting the virus to other countries. Authorities advise prevention measures such as eliminating standing water, using insect repellent, and wearing protective clothing. While no confirmed local transmission has occurred yet, the risk remains, as Spain has previously seen local dengue cases and France recently reported local chikungunya cases. The healthcare system is prepared to manage any cases that arise.
Original source text (English)
CubaHeadlines Chikungunya Surge in Spain: 20 Cases Linked to Cuba Sunday, August 23, 2026 by Sophia Martinez Chikungunya fever is transmitted by mosquito bites - Image © CiberCuba As of August 16 this year, Spain has identified 119 chikungunya cases, nearly tripling the 42 cases reported during the same period in 2025, according to the National Epidemiological Surveillance Network, as mentioned by La RazÃ3n. Among the cases with known origins, 54 came from Bolivia and 20 from Cuba. All infections are imported, with none originating in Spain. However, the rise in cases has prompted health officials to intensify monitoring of the Aedes albopictus mosquito, the primary carrier of the disease, which is prevalent in many parts of the country, particularly along the Mediterranean coast. The regions most affected include Catalonia, with 30 cases, followed by the Valencian Community with 27, and Madrid, reporting 13 cases. Concerns About Local Transmission Epidemiologists are primarily concerned about the potential for the virus to shift from imported to locally transmitted. This could happen if a local tiger mosquito bites an infected individual and then transmits the virus to a healthy person. Spain has previously recorded local transmission of dengue, and France recently informed the European Centre for Disease Prevention and Control about one local dengue case and 15 local chikungunya cases. Federico Arribas, from the Spanish Society of Epidemiology's Training Group, acknowledges the risk but emphasizes, "When such cases arise, our healthcare system manages them very effectively." Cuba's Role in the Spread Cuba's situation is crucial to understanding the origin of these imported cases. The island experienced a significant chikungunya outbreak starting in July 2025 in Matanzas, which spread to 15 of its 16 provinces. The Pan American Health Organization reported in February 2026 that there were 51,217 suspected cases, 1,959 lab-confirmed cases, and 46 deaths by the end of 2025. An additional 1,457 cases and two deaths were reported in January 2026. The scale of the outbreak positioned Cuba as a key exporter of the virus in the Western Hemisphere. The GeoSentinel network recorded 111 travelers contracting chikungunya in Cuba between September 2025 and January 2026, 64.2% of whom were potentially viremic upon returning to their home countries, highlighting the genuine risk of international spread. Prevention and Public Responsibility In January 2026, Argentina identified an imported case in a 47-year-old woman from CÃ3rdoba province who had traveled to Cuba, indicating the island was exporting the virus to other regional nations. In Spain, the tiger mosquito is active from April to November, coinciding with the peak summer travel season, marking this period as the highest risk for local transmission. With no chikungunya vaccine available, authorities advise removing standing water around homes, using insect repellent, wearing long sleeves and pants, and being especially careful at dawn and dusk. Travelers experiencing symptoms like high fever, severe joint pain, or headaches after a trip should seek medical attention and disclose their travel history. "As citizens, we have a responsibility to protect our health," Arribas advises, recommending travelers check the epidemiological status of their destination and visit an International Vaccination Center for guidance before traveling. Understanding Chikungunya and its Impact What is the main vector for chikungunya in Spain? The primary vector for chikungunya in Spain is the Aedes albopictus mosquito, also known as the tiger mosquito. How can individuals protect themselves from chikungunya? Individuals can protect themselves by eliminating standing water near their homes, using insect repellent, wearing protective clothing, and being cautious during dawn and dusk. Why is Cuba a significant concern in the spread of chikungunya? Cuba experienced a large chikungunya outbreak, making it a major source of the virus in the Western Hemisphere, which contributes to the spread through international travel.
